g_{t}(\theta)=(f^{\prime}(\theta))^{2}/(f(\theta)(1-f(\theta)))
. It is clear that Assumption
2
holds from the definitions of
\gamma_{\alpha}
and
\mu_{\alpha}
. Meanwhile, we have
\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}=L_{\alpha}^{2}/4,\textsf{Err}_{\infty}=L_{\alpha}
, and
\textsf{r}_{\textsf{2}/\infty}=1/2
. The proof is concluded by Theorem
1
.
∎

For ease of exposition, assume
m,r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},\mu_{\alpha},\gamma_{\alpha}\asymp O(1)
and
d_{j}\asymp d
for
j\in[m]
. Theorem
12
requires an upper bound of step size
\eta\leq\eta_{\textsf{comp}}:=c(d\log^{2}d)^{-1}\cdot\min\big\{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}L_{\alpha}^{-2},\log^{-1/2}d\big\}
for some small constant
c>0
. Similarly, we observe a computational and statistical trade-off. To achieve statistical optimality, given any known horizon
T
, we can set
\eta=\eta_{\textsf{stat}}:=C_{m}(T\gamma_{\alpha})^{-1}\log d
and Theorem
12
gives rise to

\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{T}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=O_{m}\bigg(d^{-C_{m}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+L_{\alpha}^{2}\cdot\frac{\overline{\textsf{dof}}\log d}{T\gamma_{\alpha}^{2}}\bigg),

which is valid when SNR
\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}/L_{\alpha}^{2}=\Omega\big(T^{-1}d\log^{3}d\big)
and horizon
T=\Omega\big(d\log^{7/2}d\big)
. If we choose a sufficiently large
C_{m}
so that the first term is negligible, we end up with an optimal error rate matching the minimax lower bound established in
Taki et al.
.

Appendix C
Efficiency of Riemannian Gradient Descent

The efficiency of RGrad lies in the step of computing HOSVD. At each step, we will need to compute the following:

\displaystyle\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}),

where
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
is the current estimator whose Tucker rank is
{\bm{r}}
, and we absorb the step-size into the gradient
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}
. Now we illustrate how we RGrad can help reduce the computational cost. We denote
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}
be its Tucker decomposition. Then (see e.g.
(
Kressner et al. 2014
)
)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}={\bm{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top})+\sum_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}\times{\bm{W}}_{i},

where
{\bm{W}}_{i}=({\bm{I}}-{\bm{U}}_{i}{\bm{U}}_{i}^{\top}){\mathcal{M}}_{i}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}})({\bm{U}}_{m}\otimes\cdots\otimes{\bm{U}}_{i+1}\otimes{\bm{U}}_{i-1}\otimes\cdots\otimes{\bm{U}}_{1}){\mathcal{M}}_{i}({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{i}\times r_{i}}
.
Then

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}

\displaystyle={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top})-\sum_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}\times{\bm{W}}_{i}

\displaystyle=({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top})\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}-\sum_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}\times{\bm{W}}_{i}

\displaystyle={\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}[{\bm{U}}_{j}\quad{\bm{W}}_{j}],

where
{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2r_{1}\times\cdots\times 2r_{m}}
such that
{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}(1:r_{1},\cdots,1:r_{m})={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}
, and
{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}(1:r_{1},\cdots,r_{j}+1:2r_{j},\cdots,1:r_{m})=-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}
for all
j\in[m]
. Now we set
[{\bm{U}}_{j}\quad{\bm{W}}_{j}]={\bm{Q}}_{j}{\bm{R}}_{j}
be its compact QR decomposition, where
{\bm{Q}}_{j}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{j},2r_{j}}
, and
{\bm{R}}_{j}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2r_{j}\times 2r_{j}}
. Then we have

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}

\displaystyle=({\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{R}}_{j})\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{Q}}_{j}.

Here
{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{R}}_{j}
is a tensor of size
2r_{1}\times\cdots\times 2r_{m}
. And let the best rank
{\bm{r}}
Tucker decomposition of it be

\displaystyle\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{R}}_{j})=\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}\tilde{\bm{U}}_{j},

where
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{1}\times\cdots\times r_{m}}
and
\tilde{\bm{U}}_{j}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{j},r_{j}}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}})=\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{Q}}_{j}\tilde{\bm{U}}_{j}).

Notice here it suffices to compute the HOSVD of a size
2{\bm{r}}
tensor instead of a size
{\bm{d}}
tensor, and this explains the efficiency of RGrad.

Computational cost analysis.
In computing the HOSVD of the gradient update, we need to compute
{\bm{W}}_{j}
, the QR decomposition of
[{\bm{U}}_{j}\quad{\bm{W}}_{j}]
, and the
\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{R}}_{j})
(including the tensor product
{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{R}}_{j}
). When
d_{1}=\cdots=d_{m}=d
, and
r_{1}=\cdots=r_{m}=r
, the total computational cost is
O(md^{m}r+dr^{m}+r^{m+1})=O(md^{m}r)
.

Alternatively, if one uses projected gradient descent, that is we need to compute
\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}})
at each time, where
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}
is a full rank tensor.
Therefore we need to compute
m
SVD of full rank matrices, which requires the computation cost of order
O(md^{m+1})
.

Appendix D
Introduction to the Representation Formula of Spectral Projectors

This section reviews the representation formula of spectral projectors. We display this section specifically for our usage in analysis. Interested readers are referred to
Xia 2021
for more details.

D.1
Symmetric Case

Let
{\bm{A}},{\bm{X}}
be
d\times d
symmetric matrices and
\text{rank}({\bm{A}})\leq r
and
{\bm{A}}
admits an eigen-decomposition

{\bm{A}}={\bm{\Theta}}{\bm{\Lambda}}{\bm{\Theta}}^{\top}
with
{\bm{\Theta}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times r}
and
{\bm{\Lambda}}=\text{diag}(\lambda_{1},\cdots,\lambda_{r})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times r}
. And the noise matrix
{\bm{X}}
satisfies
\|{\bm{X}}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}\min_{i=1}^{r}|\lambda_{i}|
. Suppose we observe
\widehat{\bm{A}}={\bm{A}}+{\bm{X}}
but
{\bm{A}},{\bm{X}}
are unknown and our aim is to estimate
{\bm{\Theta}}
. We denote
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}
the
d\times r
matrix containing the eigenvectors of
\widehat{\bm{A}}
with largest
r
eigenvalues in absolute values. Now we are able to write
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}^{\top}-{\bm{\Theta}}{\bm{\Theta}}^{\top}
explicitly. Follow Theorem 1 in
Xia 2021
, we have

\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}^{\top}-{\bm{\Theta}}{\bm{\Theta}}^{\top}=\sum_{k\geq 1}{\bm{S}}_{k},

where
{\bm{S}}_{k}
has the following form

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{k}=\sum_{s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k}(-1)^{1+\|{\bm{s}}\|_{0}}{\bm{P}}^{-s_{1}}{\bm{X}}\cdots{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-s_{k+1}}

(10)

with
{\bm{P}}^{0}={\bm{\Theta}}_{\perp}{\bm{\Theta}}_{\perp}^{\top}=:{\bm{P}}^{\perp}
and
{\bm{P}}^{-s}={\bm{\Theta}}{\bm{\Lambda}}^{-s}{\bm{\Theta}}^{\top}
for
s\geq 1
. In our problem setting only the first two terms are carefully dealt with so here we write out them explicitly

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1}

\displaystyle={\bm{P}}^{\perp}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-1}+{\bm{P}}^{-1}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{\perp},

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{2}

\displaystyle={\bm{P}}^{\perp}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-2}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}+{\bm{P}}^{-2}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}+{\bm{P}}^{\perp}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\quad-({\bm{P}}^{-1}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-1}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}+{\bm{P}}^{\perp}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-1}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-1}+{\bm{P}}^{-1}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}{\bm{X}}{\bm{P}}^{-1}).

D.2
Asymmetric Case

Let
{\bm{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
be a rank
r
matrix that admits the compact SVD
{\bm{M}}={\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{V}}^{\top}
and
{\bm{Z}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
be the noise matrix such that
\|{\bm{Z}}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{r}
. We observe
\widehat{\bm{M}}={\bm{M}}+{\bm{Z}}
but
{\bm{M}},{\bm{Z}}
are unknown and our aim is to estimate
{\bm{U}},{\bm{V}}
. Let
\widehat{\bm{U}}
and
\widehat{\bm{V}}
be
\widehat{\bm{M}}
’s top-
r
left and right singular vectors. We can reduce this case to the symmetric case using lifting. More specifically, set

\widehat{\bm{A}}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\widehat{\bm{M}}\\
\widehat{\bm{M}}^{\top}&0\\
\end{bmatrix},\quad{\bm{A}}=\begin{bmatrix}0&{\bm{M}}\\
{\bm{M}}^{\top}&0\\
\end{bmatrix},\quad{\bm{X}}=\begin{bmatrix}0&{\bm{Z}}\\
{\bm{Z}}^{\top}&0\\
\end{bmatrix}.

As a result,
\widehat{\bm{A}}
admits a decomposition,

{\bm{A}}=\begin{bmatrix}0&{\bm{U}}\\
{\bm{V}}&0\\
\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}0&{\bm{\Sigma}}\\
{\bm{\Sigma}}&0\\
\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}0&{\bm{V}}^{\top}\\
{\bm{U}}^{\top}&0\\
\end{bmatrix}.

So the projector onto the subspace spanned by non-zero eigenvectors of
{\bm{A}}
is
\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{U}}{\bm{U}}^{\top}&0\\
0&{\bm{V}}{\bm{V}}^{\top}\\
\end{bmatrix}
. So we can apply the result in the previous section, and we see

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}{\bm{U}}^{\top}&0\\
0&\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\top}-{\bm{V}}{\bm{V}}^{\top}\\
\end{bmatrix}=\sum_{k\geq 1}{\bm{S}}_{k},

where
{\bm{S}}_{k}
is defined in (
10
) with
{\bm{P}}^{0}={\bm{P}}^{\perp}=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}&0\\
0&{\bm{V}}_{\perp}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}^{\top}\\
\end{bmatrix}
and for
s\geq 1
,

{\bm{P}}^{-s}=\begin{cases}\begin{bmatrix}0&{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s}{\bm{V}}^{\top}\\
{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s}{\bm{U}}^{\top}&0\\
\end{bmatrix},&\text{if $s$ is odd},\\
\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s}{\bm{U}}^{\top}&0\\
0&{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s}{\bm{V}}^{\top}\\
\end{bmatrix},&\text{if $s$ is even}.\end{cases}

Now we take the left singular vectors as an example, that is, we are interested in
\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}{\bm{U}}^{\top}
. From the above discussion, it has the following form

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}{\bm{U}}^{\top}=\sum_{k\geq 1}{\bm{S}}_{k}^{\text{left}},

where
{\bm{S}}_{k}^{\text{left}}
is the top left block matrix of
{\bm{S}}_{k}
.
For example,

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\text{left}}

\displaystyle={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}+{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp},

(11)

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{2,1}^{\text{left}}

\displaystyle={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp},

(12)

where
{\bm{S}}_{2,1}^{\text{left}}
is the term corresponding to
{\bm{s}}=(0,2,0)
.
Then for any
k\geq 1
and
{\bm{s}}
satisfies
s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k
,
{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}})
must be of the following form

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}})={\bm{A}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{1}}{\bm{B}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{2}}\cdots{\bm{B}}_{k}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{k+1}}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{\top},

(13)

where
{\bm{A}}_{1},{\bm{A}}_{2}\in\{{\bm{U}},{\bm{U}}^{\top}\}
,
{\bm{B}}_{i}\in\{{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}},{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}},{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp},{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\text{ or their transpose}\}
, and
{\bm{\Sigma}}^{0}
is the identity matrix whose size depends on the adjacent matrices.

Appendix E
Martingale Concentration Inequality

Theorem 13

(Azuma-Hoeffding Inequality)
.

Suppose
X_{n},n\geq 1
is a martingale such that
X_{0}=0
and
|X_{i}-X_{i-1}|\leq d_{i},1\leq i\leq n
almost surely for some constant
d_{i},1\leq i\leq n
. Then for every
t\geq 0
,

{\mathbb{P}}(|X_{n}|\geq t)\leq 2\exp(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sum_{i=1}^{n}d_{i}^{2}}).

Appendix F
Technical Lemmas

The following lemma is a stronger version of Lemma 15.2 in
Cai et al. 2022a
when the perturbation has certain structure.

Lemma 1
.

Let
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}={\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\cdot({\bm{V}}_{1},\ldots,{\bm{V}}_{m})
be the tensor with Tucker rank
{\bm{r}}=(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m})
. Let
{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}={\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
be a perturbation tensor such that
8\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{D}}})\leq\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
. Then for
{\mathcal{R}}=\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}
we have

\|{\mathcal{R}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{59m\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

Proof.

We introduce some notations that will be used throughout the proof. For an orthogonal matrix
{\bm{U}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times r}
, let
{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}={\bm{U}}{\bm{U}}^{\top}
be the projector and
{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times(d-r)}
be the orthogonal complement of
{\bm{U}}
and
{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}={\bm{U}}_{\perp}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}
. For a tensor
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
, we use
{\bm{T}}_{i}={\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(i)}
to denote its
i
-th unfolding.

Without loss of generality, we prove the lemma when
m=3
. First notice

{\mathcal{R}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}})=({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}})\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{3}}),

where
{\bm{U}}_{i}
are the leading
r_{i}
left singular vectors of
{\bm{T}}_{i}+{\bm{D}}_{i}
. Following Theorem 1 in
Xia 2021
, we have for all
i
,

{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{i}}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{i}}={\bm{S}}_{i,1}+\sum_{k\geq 2}{\bm{S}}_{i,k},

where
{\bm{S}}_{i,1}=({\bm{T}}_{i}^{\top})^{\dagger}{\bm{D}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{i}}^{\perp}+{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{i}}^{\perp}{\bm{D}}_{i}{\bm{T}}_{i}^{\dagger}
and
{\bm{S}}_{i,k}
satisfy
\|{\bm{S}}_{i,k}\|\leq(\frac{4\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{D}}})}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{k}
and
the explicit form of
{\bm{S}}_{i,k}
can be found in
Xia 2021
. Here we denote
{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}
the pseudo-inverse of
{\bm{A}}
, i.e.,
{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}={\bm{R}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{L}}^{\top}
given the compact SVD of
{\bm{A}}={\bm{L}}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{R}}^{\top}
.

For the sake of brevity, we denote
{\bm{S}}_{i}=\sum_{k\geq 1}{\bm{S}}_{i,k}
. As a result of
8\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{D}}})\leq\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
, we see

\|{\bm{S}}_{i}\|\leq\sum_{k\geq 1}(\frac{4\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{D}}})}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{k}\leq\frac{8\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{D}}})}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\quad{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{3}})={\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}+{\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}+{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}+{\bm{S}}_{3})

\displaystyle=\underbrace{{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})}_{\text{zeroth order term}={\bm{\mathcal{T}}}}+\underbrace{{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{S}}_{3})}_{\text{first order term}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{S}}_{3})+{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{S}}_{3})}_{\text{second order term}}+\underbrace{{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{S}}_{3})}_{\text{third order term}}.

(14)

Now we consider
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{3}})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
. The zeroth order term is exactly
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
. For the first order term, we take
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})
as an example. And we consider
{\mathcal{M}}_{1}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}))
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}_{1}\big({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})\big)

\displaystyle={\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{T}}_{1}={\bm{S}}_{1,1}{\bm{T}}_{1}+\sum_{k\geq 2}{\bm{S}}_{1,k}{\bm{T}}_{1}

\displaystyle={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp}{\bm{D}}_{1}{\bm{T}}_{1}^{\dagger}{\bm{T}}_{1}+\sum_{k\geq 2}{\bm{S}}_{1,k}{\bm{T}}_{1}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{M}}_{1}\big({\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})\big)+\sum_{k\geq 2}{\bm{S}}_{1,k}{\bm{T}}_{1},

(15)

where in the second line we use the fact
{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp}{\bm{T}}_{1}=\bm{0}
.
This implies

\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{T}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}
and
meanwhile

\|\sum_{k\geq 2}{\bm{S}}_{1,k}{\bm{T}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\sum_{k\geq 2}(\frac{4\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{D}}})}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{k}\leq 32\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}.

For the second order term, we take
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})
as an example and consider
{\mathcal{M}}_{1}\big({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})\big)
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{M}}_{1}\big({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})\big)\|_{\rm{F}}=\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{T}}_{1}({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}\otimes{\bm{S}}_{2})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{T}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}\otimes{\bm{S}}_{2}\|\leq\frac{16\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

(16)

For the third order term, we can similarly show
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{S}}_{3})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}
. Putting this and (
F
) - (
16
) together and we see

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{3}})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}

\displaystyle={\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})

\displaystyle\quad+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}^{\perp})+{\bm{\mathcal{R}}}_{1}

(17)

with the remainder satisfying
\|{\bm{\mathcal{R}}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{49m\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}
. We now consider
{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{3}})
. Expanding this gives us

\displaystyle\quad{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{3}})={\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}+{\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}+{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}+{\bm{S}}_{3})

\displaystyle={\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{S}}_{3})

\displaystyle\quad+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{S}}_{3})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{S}}_{3})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2},{\bm{S}}_{3})

\displaystyle=:{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{R}}}_{2}.

(18)

Using a similar idea we can show that
\|{\bm{\mathcal{R}}}_{2}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{10m\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}
.
Finally since
{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}={\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}})
has the following form,

{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}={\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}})={\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\times_{i=1}^{3}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\times_{j\in[3]\backslash i}{\bm{V}}_{j}\times_{i}{\bm{W}}_{i}.

Therefore
{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})={\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}^{\perp})=\bm{0}
.
Notice

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}

\displaystyle={\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}+{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}+{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}+{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}^{\perp})={\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})

\displaystyle\quad+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}}^{\perp},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}})+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\cdot({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{1}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{2}},{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{V}}_{3}}^{\perp}).

(19)

From (
F
),(
F
) and (
F
), we see

\displaystyle{\mathcal{R}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}={\bm{\mathcal{R}}}_{1}+{\bm{\mathcal{R}}}_{2},

which implies
\|{\mathcal{R}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}+{\bm{\mathcal{D}}})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 59m\frac{\|{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}
.
∎

Lemma 2
.

Let
{\bm{X}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
be the random matrix with i.i.d. standard normal entries. Then for any orthogonal matrices
{\bm{U}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times r_{1}}
,
{\bm{V}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{2}\times r_{2}}
,

\big\|\|{\bm{U}}^{T}{\bm{X}}{\bm{V}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim r_{1}r_{2}.

Proof.

First we have
\|{\bm{U}}^{T}{\bm{X}}{\bm{V}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq r_{1}\wedge r_{2}\|{\bm{U}}^{T}{\bm{X}}{\bm{V}}\|
. Now the result follows that
{\bm{U}}^{T}{\bm{X}}{\bm{V}}
has the same distribution as a matrix of size
r_{1}\times r_{2}
with i.i.d. standard normal entries, whose
\psi_{1}
norm is bounded by
O(r_{1}\vee r_{2})
.
∎

Lemma 3
.

Let
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot({\bm{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\bm{U}}_{m})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
be a Tucker rank
{\bm{r}}
tensor. Let
{\mathbb{T}}
be its corresponding tangent plane. Let
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
be the random tensor having i.i.d. standard normal entries that is independent of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
. Then

\big\|\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

Proof.

Notice
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}
must be of the following form:

{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}={\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}\times{\bm{W}}_{i},

where
{\bm{W}}_{i}=\big({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}\times_{i}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{i}}^{\perp}\big)_{(i)}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(i)}^{\dagger}
. This implies each of the
m+1
components are mutually orthogonal under the standard inner product. As a result,

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{i}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}\times{\bm{W}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

For the first term, we have
\|{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{i}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|{\bm{U}}_{1}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{(1)}({\bm{U}}_{m}\otimes\cdots\otimes{\bm{U}}_{2})^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.
Then from Lemma
2
, we have

\big\|\|{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{i}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim r^{*}.

On the other hand, for each
i\in[m]
,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}\times{\bm{W}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{W}}_{i}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(i)}(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\bm{U}}_{j})^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{i}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{(i)}(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\bm{U}}_{j}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(i)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(i)}(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\bm{U}}_{j})^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{(i)}{\bm{O}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where
{\bm{O}}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{i}^{-}\times r_{i}}
is an orthogonal matrix since
(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\bm{U}}_{j}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(i)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(i)}(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\bm{U}}_{j})^{\top}
is a projector. And therefore

\big\|\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\in[m]\backslash i}{\bm{U}}_{j}\times{\bm{W}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim d_{i}r_{i}.

Using a triangular inequality and we get the desired result.
∎

Lemma 4

(Lemma 3.2 of
Koch and Lubich 2010
)
.

Let
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
be a Tucker rank
{\bm{r}}
tensor. Let
{\mathbb{T}}
be its corresponding tangent plane. Then for any
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
such that
\|{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})}{16m(m+3)}
we have

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{8m(m+3)}{\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Lemma 5

(Incoherence implies spikiness)
.

Let
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
be a Tucker rank
{\bm{r}}
tensor with the decomposition
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}
. Denote the condition number of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
as
\kappa_{0}=\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})/\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})
. Suppose
\textsf{Incoh}({\bm{U}}_{j})\leq\mu_{0}
, then
\textsf{Spiki}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})\leq\sqrt{r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\kappa_{0}\cdot\mu_{0}^{m/2}
, where
r^{*}=r_{1}\cdots r_{m}
,
r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}=\max_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}
.

Proof.

Notice that

\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})\prod_{j=1}^{m}\|{\bm{U}}_{j}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})\prod_{j=1}^{m}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}.

On the other hand,

\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})
. Therefore

\textsf{Spiki}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})\leq\sqrt{r_{1}\cdots r_{m}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\kappa_{0}\cdot\mu_{0}^{m/2}.

∎

Lemma 6
.

Let
{\bm{M}}_{1},{\bm{M}}_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
be two rank
r
matrices. Denote
\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}=\min\{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}({\bm{M}}_{1}),\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}({\bm{M}}_{2})\}
and
\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}=\max\{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}({\bm{M}}_{1}),\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}({\bm{M}}_{2})\}
. Suppose
\|{\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
, then

\|{\bm{M}}_{1}^{\dagger}-{\bm{M}}_{2}^{\dagger}\|\leq(4\sqrt{2}+1)\frac{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{3}}\|{\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}\|.

Proof.

Let
{\bm{M}}_{1}={\bm{U}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}{\bm{V}}_{1}^{\top}
, be the compact SVD of
{\bm{M}}_{1}
and

{\bm{M}}_{2}={\bm{U}}_{2}^{\prime}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{\prime}{\bm{V}}_{2}^{\prime\top}

be the compact SVD of
{\bm{M}}_{1},{\bm{M}}_{2}
.
Define

\displaystyle{\bm{L}}=\arg\min_{{\bm{L}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r}}\|{\bm{U}}_{1}-{\bm{U}}_{2}^{\prime}{\bm{L}}\|,\quad{\bm{R}}=\arg\min_{{\bm{R}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r}}\|{\bm{V}}_{1}-{\bm{V}}_{2}^{\prime}R\|,

where
{\mathbb{O}}_{r}
is the set of all orthogonal matrices of size
r\times r
.
Denote
{\bm{U}}_{2}={\bm{U}}_{2}^{\prime}{\bm{L}},{\bm{V}}_{2}={\bm{V}}_{2}^{\prime}{\bm{R}},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}={\bm{L}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{\prime}{\bm{R}}
. Then
{\bm{M}}_{2}={\bm{U}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}{\bm{V}}_{2}^{\top}

but
{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}
is not necessarily diagonal.
As a result, we can write
{\bm{M}}_{1}^{\dagger}={\bm{V}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top},{\bm{M}}_{2}^{\dagger}={\bm{V}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}{\bm{U}}_{2}^{\top}
.
Now since
\|{\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
, using Wedin’s sin
\Theta
Theorem, we obtain

\displaystyle\max\{\|{\bm{U}}_{1}-{\bm{U}}_{2}\|,\|{\bm{V}}_{1}-{\bm{V}}_{2}\|\}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\|{\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}\|}{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

(20)

We first bound
\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}\|
. In fact,

\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}

\displaystyle={\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}{\bm{V}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{V}}_{1}-{\bm{U}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}{\bm{V}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{V}}_{2}

\displaystyle=({\bm{U}}_{1}-{\bm{U}}_{2})^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{V}}_{1}+{\bm{U}}_{2}^{\top}({\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}){\bm{V}}_{1}+{\bm{U}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{2}({\bm{V}}_{1}-{\bm{V}}_{2}).

Using triangle inequality and (
20
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}\|\leq(2\sqrt{2}+1)\frac{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}\|.

Next we bound
\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}\|
using this inequality,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}\|=\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}){\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}\|\leq\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}\|\leq(2\sqrt{2}+1)\frac{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{\sigma^{3}_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}\|.

(21)

Now we are ready to bound
\|{\bm{M}}_{1}^{\dagger}-{\bm{M}}_{2}^{\dagger}\|
,

\displaystyle{\bm{M}}_{1}^{\dagger}-{\bm{M}}_{2}^{\dagger}={\bm{V}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\bm{V}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}{\bm{U}}_{2}^{\top}=({\bm{V}}_{1}-{\bm{V}}_{2}){\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}+{\bm{V}}_{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{1}^{-1}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}){\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}+{\bm{V}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{2}^{-1}({\bm{U}}_{1}-{\bm{U}}_{2})^{\top}.

Using triangle inequality, (
20
) and (
21
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\bm{M}}_{1}^{\dagger}-{\bm{M}}_{2}^{\dagger}\|\leq(4\sqrt{2}+1)\frac{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{3}}\|{\bm{M}}_{1}-{\bm{M}}_{2}\|.

∎

Appendix G
Proofs

G.1
Proof of Theorem
1

Define the event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t}=\bigg\{\forall 0\leq l\leq t,\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2},|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\rangle|\leq C\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},\quad|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg\}.

Then under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
and Assumption
3
,

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\leq\alpha,

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C\big(\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha,

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim\alpha^{2}
.

Step 1: Relation between
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})
and
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})
.

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})=\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}+{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad\leq(1+\delta^{-1})\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})+(1+\delta)\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t}).

(22)

Since
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}=h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
, and
|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|,|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq\alpha
, from Assumption
2
,

\displaystyle|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|

\displaystyle\leq|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})-h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|+|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{\alpha}|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|+|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|,

(23)

we obtain under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(\mu_{\alpha}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|\bigg)(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(\mu_{\alpha}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\textsf{Err}_{\infty}\bigg)(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}.

Under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
, this implies

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mu_{\alpha}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{1/2}\big(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+(\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2})^{1/2}\big)+C\textsf{Err}_{\infty}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\mu_{\alpha}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{1/2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+C\textsf{Err}_{\infty}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2},

as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\mu_{\alpha}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}}{\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}}\lesssim 1
.
Now as long as
\eta\mu_{\alpha}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
and
\eta\textsf{Err}_{\infty}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}\lesssim\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
,

\eta\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
, we use Lemma
1
with
{\bm{\mathcal{D}}}=-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}),{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}={\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}
, and we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})\leq C_{m}\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\eta^{4}\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\cdot\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})+C_{m}\eta^{4}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{2}}{12}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})+C_{m}\eta^{4}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2},

as long as
\eta^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-2}\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\log^{4}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\lesssim 1
and
\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-3}\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{3}\log^{5}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\lesssim 1
.

Lower bound for
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})
.

We denote

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2},|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\rangle|\leq C\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},\quad|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg\}.

(24)

And
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}=\nabla l({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},\mathfrak{D}_{t})=h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
. Then from Assumption
3
,
{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}=0
. Since
{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}
is independent of
Y_{t}
,
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})=0
.
So we have
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*})\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})=0
since
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}
is linear operator and the expectation is taken with respect to
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},Y_{t}
following the definition of
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}
.
Then
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})=\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})\cdot{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})
. Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t}).

And since
|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|,|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq\alpha
, using Assumption
2
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle\big(h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})-h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})\big)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle\geq\gamma_{\alpha}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle=\gamma_{\alpha}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle^{2}\cdot\big(1-\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})\big)

\displaystyle\geq\gamma_{\alpha}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\gamma_{\alpha}\big({\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle^{4}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{4}{5}\gamma_{\alpha}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality holds since
{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})\leq 14d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
. On the other hand,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t}|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})\rangle|\cdot|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})-h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|

\displaystyle\leq C\mu_{\alpha}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C_{m}\mu_{\alpha}\frac{\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

Now as long as

\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\gamma_{\alpha}}{\mu_{\alpha}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
and

\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\frac{\mu_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}{\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}}
,
we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})\geq\frac{3}{4}\gamma_{\alpha}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}).

(25)

We derive the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})
. In fact, from (
G.1
) and Lemma
3
, and
|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq\alpha
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})\big(\mu_{\alpha}^{2}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle^{2}+h^{2}_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})\big)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})\big(\mu_{\alpha}^{2}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{t}}h^{2}_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\overline{\textsf{dof}}\mu_{\alpha}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+C\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2},

where in the last inequality is from the definition of
\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}
.
And therefore

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})-2\eta\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})+\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle=\bigg(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-2\eta\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})+\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{3}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+C\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}(\mu_{\alpha}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-2\eta\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+C\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-2\eta\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t}),

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\frac{\mu_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\lesssim 1
.
Now we set
\delta=\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha}
in (
G.1
), and we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+C\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\bigg({\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})+C_{m}\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+C\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\bigg({\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t}),

where the last inequality holds as long as
\frac{\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}}{\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}}\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim_{m}1
. Telescoping this and we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})\leq(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{t-l}(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})}_{=:D_{l}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{t-l}2\eta(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\bigg({\mathbb{E}}_{l}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle\bigg)\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})}_{=:F_{l}}.

Step 2: Martingale concentration inequality.

Now we use Azuma-Hoeffding inequality (c.f. Theorem
13
) to bound
|\sum_{l}D_{l}|
and
|\sum_{l}F_{l}|
. Notice under the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{l}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq C\bigg(\mu_{\alpha}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\bigg)(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}).

And therefore

\displaystyle|D_{l}|\leq C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{t-l}\eta^{2}\bigg(\mu_{\alpha}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\bigg)(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})=:d_{l}.

And

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}d_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=0}^{t}C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{2t-2l}\eta^{4}\bigg(\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\cdot\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4}\bigg)(\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\cdot\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=0}^{t}C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{2t-2l}\eta^{4}\bigg(\mu_{\alpha}^{4}(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{2l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\cdot\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4}\bigg)(\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\cdot\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})

\displaystyle\leq C\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\log^{4}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{2t+2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+C\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\cdot\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4},

where the second inequality holds as long as
\frac{\textsf{Err}_{2}^{4}}{\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4}}\eta^{2}\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
. So with probability exceeding
1-2d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle|\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}|

\displaystyle\leq C(\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\log^{5}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{2t+2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4})^{1/2}+C(\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\cdot\log^{3}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2},

(26)

as long as
\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\mu_{\alpha}^{4}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\log^{5}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
and
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}\frac{\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{4}}{\textsf{Err}_{2}^{4}}\log^{3}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
. On the other hand,

\displaystyle\quad|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})|=|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{*})\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})|+|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{*}\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|\cdot|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle,Y_{l})|\cdot|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+C\textsf{Err}_{\infty}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l}).

And thus

\displaystyle|F_{l}|\leq C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{t-l}\eta\bigg(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+\textsf{Err}_{\infty}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})\bigg)=:f_{l}.

Also,

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}f_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=0}^{t}C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}\bigg(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\cdot\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})+\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{l})\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=0}^{t}C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{4})^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}\bigg[(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{2l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\eta^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{4}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{2t+2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C\eta^{3}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-3}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{4}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\quad+C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t+1}\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C\eta^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{2t+2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t+1}\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\eta^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

where the last line holds as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{-2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
. Now using the inequality
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
to the second term and we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}f_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{2t+2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1}+C\eta^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\log^{3}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
and
\frac{\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}}{\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim\overline{\textsf{dof}}
. So with probability exceeding
1-2d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle|\sum_{l=0}^{t}F_{l}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{1/2}\textsf{Err}_{2}\textsf{Err}_{\infty}\log^{3/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2},

(27)

where the last inequality holds as long as
\frac{\textsf{Err}_{\infty}^{2}}{\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim\overline{\textsf{dof}}
. Now from (
G.1
) and (
G.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

Step 3: Controlling the probability.

Now we bound the probability of the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{T}
. Notice

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{t}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{t+1}^{c})\leq{\mathbb{P}}\bigg({\mathcal{E}}_{t}\cap\{\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\geq 2(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{8})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}.\}\bigg)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t+1}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t+1})\|_{\rm{F}}\gtrsim\sqrt{\overline{\textsf{dof}}\log d}\bigg)+{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t+1},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle|\gtrsim\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\log d}\bigg)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t+1},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t+1}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\rangle|\gtrsim\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t+1}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\log d}\bigg)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t+1}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)+{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t+1}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 14d^{-10},

where the last inequality holds from Lemma
3
, and the other four are Gaussian random variables since
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
is independent of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
.
And thus

{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{T}^{c})=\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{c})\leq 14Td^{-10}.

G.2
Proof of Theorem
3

Proof.

Notice for each
j\in[m]
,
\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}
is actually the top
r_{j}
left singular vectors of the following matrix:

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{N}}_{j}=\frac{1}{T_{1}(T_{1}-1)}\sum_{1\leq i<i^{\prime}\leq T_{1}}Y_{i}Y_{i^{\prime}}({\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}})^{\top}+{\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}).

We denote
{\bm{N}}_{j}={\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})^{\top}
.
Using Wedin’s sin
\Theta
theorem, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}.

Now
\widehat{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}
is a U-statistics of order 2, using standard decoupling techniques for U-statistics (see e.g. Theorem 3.4.1 in
De la Pena and Giné 2012
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(\|\widehat{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|\geq t)\leq 15{\mathbb{P}}(\|\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|\geq t),

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}=\frac{1}{2T_{1}(T_{1}-1)}\sum_{i\neq i^{\prime}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i^{\prime}}({\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}})^{\top}+{\mathcal{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}),

with
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},\tilde{Y}_{i}
i.i.d. copy of
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},Y_{i}
such that

\displaystyle\tilde{Y}_{i}=\langle\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle+\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}.

For notation simplicity, we drop the subscript
j
, and we denote
m_{1}=d_{j}
,
m_{2}=d_{j}^{-}
,
{\bm{M}}={\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{1}\times m_{2}}
, and
{\bm{X}}_{i}={\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})
.
We define

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1}=\bm{\Delta}_{1}+{\bm{Z}}_{1},\quad{\bm{S}}_{2}=\bm{\Delta}_{2}+{\bm{Z}}_{2},

where

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{1}=\left(\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\langle{\bm{X}}_{i},{\bm{M}}\rangle{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}\right),

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{2}=\left(\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\langle\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i},{\bm{M}}\rangle\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}\right),

\displaystyle{\bm{Z}}_{1}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\epsilon_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i},

\displaystyle{\bm{Z}}_{2}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}.

Recall we write
{\bm{N}}_{j}={\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}
and thus

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{T_{1}}{2(T_{1}-1)}({\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}+{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top})+\frac{T_{1}}{2(T_{1}-1)}({\bm{S}}_{1}+{\bm{S}}_{2}){\bm{M}}^{\top}+\frac{T_{1}}{2(T_{1}-1)}{\bm{M}}({\bm{S}}_{1}+{\bm{S}}_{2})^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{T_{1}-1}\left(\frac{1}{2T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i}({\bm{X}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}+\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top})-{\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\right).

We denote
M=\max\{m_{1},m_{2}\}
.
Using matrix Bernstein inequality (see e.g.
Koltchinskii et al. 2011
) and Lemma 2.1 in
Koltchinskii and Xia 2016
, we have the following event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{1}=\bigg\{\max\{\|{\bm{Z}}_{1}\|,\|{\bm{Z}}_{2}\|\}\leq C\sigma\frac{\sqrt{M}\log M}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\bigg\}

holds with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
. And from matrix Bernstein inequality,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{2}=\bigg\{\max\{\|\bm{\Delta}_{1}\|,\|\bm{\Delta}_{2}\|\}\leq\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}\frac{\sqrt{M}\log M}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\bigg\}

holds with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
. We now proceed our proof conditioning on
{\mathcal{E}}_{1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{2}
.

Upper bound for
\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}\|,\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|
.

We only consider the upper bound for
\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|
. Notice
{\bm{S}}_{2}
is independent of
Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}
. We shall proceed conditioning on
{\bm{S}}_{2}
. In fact,

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}(Y_{i}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{M}}^{\top}),

and that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\bm{S}}_{2}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}\preccurlyeq\sigma^{2}m_{2}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}+3m_{1}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\preccurlyeq\sigma^{2}\textsf{Tr}({\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}){\bm{I}}_{m_{1}}+3\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\textsf{Tr}({\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}){\bm{I}}_{m_{1}}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\quad\bigg\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\bigg\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}(\langle{\bm{X}}_{i},{\bm{M}}\rangle{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}+\bigg\|\epsilon_{i}\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\langle{\bm{X}}_{i},{\bm{M}}\rangle\|_{\psi_{2}}\cdot\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\|\|_{\psi_{2}}+\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|+\|\epsilon_{i}\|_{\psi_{2}}\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\|\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}+\sigma)\cdot\sqrt{m_{1}}\|{\bm{S}}_{2}\|,

where in the last line we use the fact that
\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\|\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{m_{1}}\|{\bm{S}}_{2}\|
(see
Vershynin 2011
).
Now using the matrix Bernstein inequality (see e.g. Proposition 2 in
Koltchinskii and Xia 2016
), we have with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
, the following event holds

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{3}=\bigg\{\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|\lesssim(\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}+\sigma)\cdot\|{\bm{S}}_{2}\|\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}}}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\log M\bigg\}.

And thus under
{\mathcal{E}}_{1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{2}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{3}
,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|\lesssim(\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\cdot\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}M}}{T_{1}}\log^{2}M.

Upper bound for
\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|,\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|
.

Notice

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}.

And it is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\quad\max\bigg\{\big\|{\mathbb{E}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})\big\|,\big\|{\mathbb{E}}{\bm{M}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}\big\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim m_{1}(\sigma^{2}+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\|{\bm{M}}\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle\quad\bigg\|\|(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\|\epsilon_{i}\|_{\psi_{2}}\|\|{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|\|_{\psi_{2}}+\|{\bm{M}}\|^{2}+\|\langle{\bm{X}}_{i},{\bm{M}}\rangle\|_{\psi_{2}}\|\|{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{m_{1}}(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\|{\bm{M}}\|.

Using matrix Bernstein inequality again, and we see with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
, the following event holds,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{4}=\bigg\{\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|\lesssim(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\|{\bm{M}}\|\sqrt{\frac{m_{1}}{T_{1}}}\log M\bigg\}.

Upper bound for
\frac{1}{T_{1}-1}\left(\frac{1}{2T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i}({\bm{X}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}+\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top})-{\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\right)
.

In fact, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})+\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}.

Here the second term is
{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}
, which is just bounded above. Now we conditioned on
Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i},i=1,\cdots,T_{1}
. One can similarly show

\displaystyle\bigg\|\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim\sqrt{m_{1}}(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\|.

And

\displaystyle\quad\max\bigg\{\big\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\big\|,\big\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})\big\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim m_{1}(\sigma^{2}+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\max\bigg\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\right\|,\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{i}\right\|\bigg\}.

And we have from matrix Bernstein inequality again, and we see with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
, the following event holds,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{5}=\bigg\{

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\lesssim\sqrt{m_{1}}(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\frac{\log M}{T_{1}}\max_{i}\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{m_{1}}(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\frac{\sqrt{\log M}}{T_{1}}\sqrt{\max\bigg\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\right\|,\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{i}\right\|\bigg\}}\bigg\}.

Now we consider the following event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{6}=\bigg\{\max_{i}\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\|\lesssim\sqrt{M}(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\log T_{1}\bigg\}.

Since
\|\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\|\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim\sqrt{M}(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})
,
{\mathcal{E}}_{6}
holds with probability exceeding
1-T_{1}^{-10}
.
So we conclude on
{\mathcal{E}}_{5}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\lesssim(\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{m_{1}M\log M\log^{2}T_{1}}{T_{1}}}

Finalize the proof for subspace.

Under
\bigcap_{k=1}^{6}{\mathcal{E}}_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sigma^{2}+\|{\bm{M}}^{2}\|_{\rm{F}})\frac{(m_{1}M)^{1/2}\log^{2}M}{T_{1}}+(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\|{\bm{M}}\|\sqrt{\frac{m_{1}}{T_{1}}}\log M

\displaystyle\quad+(\sigma^{2}+\|{\bm{M}}^{2}\|_{\rm{F}})\frac{(m_{1}M\log M\log^{2}T_{1})^{1/2}}{T_{1}^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sigma^{2}+\|{\bm{M}}^{2}\|_{\rm{F}})\frac{(m_{1}M)^{1/2}\log^{2}M}{T_{1}}+(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\|{\bm{M}}\|\sqrt{\frac{m_{1}}{T_{1}}}\log M

as long as
T_{1}\geq\log^{2}T_{1}
. We now plug in the
m_{1},m_{2},M
and we obtain

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\sigma^{2}+\|{\bm{M}}^{2}\|_{\rm{F}})\frac{\big((d^{*})^{1/2}+d_{j}\big)\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T_{1}}+(\sigma+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}})\|{\bm{M}}\|\sqrt{\frac{d_{j}}{T_{1}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

where
\lesssim_{m}
hides constant depending only on the dimension
m
of the tensor. Finally, we conclude with probability exceeding
1-6d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\sigma^{2}/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2})\frac{\big((d^{*})^{1/2}+d_{j}\big)\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}+\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\frac{d_{j}}{T_{1}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

Estimation of core tensor.

Now we consider the accuracy for the core tensor estimation.
For notation simplicity, we shall use
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
instead of
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t-T_{1}}
and then
\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j},j\in[m]
is independent of
\{{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T_{2}}
. We denote the loss function

\displaystyle L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})=\frac{1}{2T_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{2}}\big(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{1}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{1}\cdots\times_{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{m}\rangle-Y_{t}\big)^{2}.

And simple computation shows
\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})=\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{2}}\big(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\rangle-Y_{t}\big){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j=1}^{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}
, and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}).

It would also be helpful to notice
\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})=0
since
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}
is the least square estimator.
We now decompose

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle=\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})-{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})-\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})\rangle}_{=:\beta_{1}}-\underbrace{\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\rangle}_{\beta_{2}}.

Using the expression for
{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{C}})
and
\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})
above, we can further decompose
\beta_{1}
as

\displaystyle\beta_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle+\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\epsilon_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle.

And we have

\displaystyle\beta_{1,1}

\displaystyle:=\bigg|-\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt+\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle=|\langle\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}{\bm{g}}_{i}{\bm{g}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{I}},{\bm{a}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\rangle|,

where
{\bm{g}}_{i}=\textsf{Vec}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r^{*}}
satisfies
\|\langle{\bm{g}}_{i},{\bm{m}}\rangle\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq C_{0}\|{\bm{m}}\|_{\ell_{2}}
(notice we here implicitly use the fact
\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}
and
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}
are independent), and

{\bm{a}}=\textsf{Vec}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}),{\bm{b}}=\textsf{Vec}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\big).

Using standard
\epsilon
-net argument, and we can see with probability exceeding
1-e^{-\delta_{1}}
(for some
\delta_{1}\leq T_{2}
to be specified),

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}{\bm{g}}_{i}{\bm{g}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{I}}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}{\bm{g}}_{i}{\bm{g}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{I}},{\bm{a}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}\|{\bm{a}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\|_{*}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}.

We have

\displaystyle\quad\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})=\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}+{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})
