\displaystyle=\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\bm{I}}_{r_{1}}\cdots\times_{j}({\bm{I}}_{r_{j}}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\times_{j+1}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{*})\cdots\times_{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{m}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{m}^{*}).

(28)

Therefore

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|{\bm{I}}_{r_{j}}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}.

So we conclude

\displaystyle\beta_{1,1}\leq\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\bigg(\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}\bigg).

On the other hand, using similar
\epsilon
-net argument, we conclude with probability exceeding
1-e^{-\delta_{1}}
,

\displaystyle\beta_{1,2}

\displaystyle:=\bigg|\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\epsilon_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle\bigg|\leq\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}\sigma\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

For
\beta_{2}
, using once again the decomposition in (
G.2
),

\displaystyle|\beta_{2}|

\displaystyle=|\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\rangle=\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle|

\displaystyle=\big|\sum_{j=1}^{m}\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\bm{I}}_{r_{1}}\cdots\times_{j}({\bm{I}}_{r_{j}}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\times_{j+1}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{*})\cdots\times_{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{m}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{m}^{*})\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Putting everything together and we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\bigg(\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}+\sigma\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}.

As a result, as long as
\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}\leq\frac{1}{2}
we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}+\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+\delta_{1}}{T_{2}}}\sigma

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{m}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}

under the given sample size condition and SNR condition.
∎

G.3
Proof of Theorem
4

Since
h_{\theta}(\theta,y)=-I^{-1}y+e^{\theta}
and
g_{t}(\theta)=I^{-1}e^{\theta}
,
Assumption
2
holds with
\gamma_{\alpha}=e^{-\alpha}
and
\mu_{\alpha}=e^{\alpha}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\max_{t=0}^{T}|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle,Y_{t})|=I^{-1}\max_{t=0}^{T}|Y_{t}-I\exp(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle)|.

Poisson distribution is sub-exponential (see Lemma
7
) and
\|Y_{t}-I\exp(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle)\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\log^{-1}(2I^{-1}e^{-\alpha}+1)
, implying that
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}=\bar{C}_{1}I^{-1}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
with probability exceeding
1-2Td_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
, where
\bar{C}_{1}=\log^{-1}(2I^{-1}e^{-\alpha}+1)
holds. Similarly, by definition,
\textsf{Err}_{2}^{2}=\max_{t=0}^{T}\max_{|\theta|\leq\alpha}g_{t}(\theta)=I^{-1}e^{\alpha}.

Consequently,
\textsf{r}_{\textsf{2}/\infty}=\bar{c}_{1}I^{1/2}\log^{-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
with
\bar{c}_{1}=e^{\alpha/2}\log^{-1}(2I^{-1}e^{-\alpha}+1)
. The proof is concluded by Theorem
1
.

G.4
Proof of Theorem
5

We first state two lemmas that are useful in the proof.

Lemma 7
.

Let
W\sim\text{Pois}(\nu)
, then
\|{W-\nu}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\frac{1}{\log(\frac{2}{\nu}+1)}
.

Proof.

This is as a result of
{\mathbb{E}}e^{\lambda(W-\nu)}=\exp\big(\nu(e^{\lambda}-\lambda-1)\big)
. And if we set
\lambda=\log(\frac{2}{\nu}+1)
,
{\mathbb{E}}e^{\lambda(W-\nu)}=2
. The result is then from the equivalent definition of
\psi_{1}
norm.
∎

Lemma 8
.

Let
W\sim\text{Pois}(\nu)
and
W^{\prime}=W\mathds{1}(\frac{1}{10}\nu\leq W\leq 10\nu)+\nu\mathds{1}(W\notin[\frac{1}{10}\nu,10\nu])
.
Then there exists absolute constant
C_{0}
such that for all
\nu\geq C_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-\log\nu\big)^{2}\leq\frac{C}{\nu}

for some absolute constant
C>0
.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-\log\nu\big)^{2}={\mathbb{E}}\big(\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-{\mathbb{E}}\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})\big)^{2}+\big({\mathbb{E}}\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-\log\nu\big)^{2}.

Using Lemma B3 in
Shi et al. 2022
, we have

\displaystyle\|\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-{\mathbb{E}}\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{\nu}}.

And thus

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-{\mathbb{E}}\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})\big)^{2}\leq\frac{C}{\nu}.

On the other hand, from Lemma B1 in
Shi et al. 2022
, we have

\displaystyle\big({\mathbb{E}}\log(W^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-\log\nu\big)^{2}\leq\frac{C}{\nu}.

These together give the desired result.
∎

Proof of Theorem
5
. .

We consider the following event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{1}=\bigg\{|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle|\leq\underbrace{C_{0}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}_{:=\tau_{0}},\quad\forall t\in[T]\bigg\},

which holds with probability exceeding
1-Td_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-100}
.
Also notice form Assumption
3
,
\tau_{0}\leq\alpha
.
We denote
\nu_{t}=I\exp(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle)
.
Moreover we set
Y_{t}^{\prime}=Y_{t}\mathds{1}(\frac{1}{10}\nu_{t}\leq Y_{t}\leq 10\nu_{t})+\nu_{t}\mathds{1}(Y_{t}\notin[\frac{1}{10}\nu_{t},10\nu_{t}])
. We also consider the event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{2}=\bigg\{Y_{t}=Y_{t}^{\prime},\quad\forall t\in[T]\bigg\}.

Then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(Y_{t}\neq Y_{t}^{\prime})\leq{\mathbb{P}}(Y_{t}\geq 10\nu_{t})+{\mathbb{P}}(Y_{t}\leq\frac{1}{10}\nu_{t}).

And

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(Y_{t}\geq 10\nu_{t})

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{P}}(Y_{t}\geq 10\nu_{t}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1})+{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{1}^{c})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{P}}\big(Y_{t}\geq 10\nu_{t}\big|\nu_{t}\in[I\exp(-\tau_{0}),I\exp(\tau_{0})]\big)+{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{1}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq\exp\big(-I\exp(-\tau_{0})\big)+Td_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-100}

\displaystyle\leq 2Td_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-100}.

where second inequality is from the tail probability of Poisson (see e.g. Lemma E.8 in
Han et al. 2022b
), and the last inequality holds as long as
I\geq C_{0}\exp(\tau_{0})\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
. We can similarly bound the second term using again Lemma E.8 in
Han et al. 2022b
, and we conclude
{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{2})\geq 1-4Td_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-100}
. Now we proceed under the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{2}
. Under these events, we have

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{t}-{\bm{T}}^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{t}-{\mathbb{E}}\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|{\mathbb{E}}\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{1}-{\bm{T}}^{*}\|,

where
{\bm{T}}^{*}={\mathcal{M}}_{{\mathcal{I}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
.
Also, we have

\displaystyle\big|\log\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}\big|\leq\big|\log\frac{10Ie^{\tau_{0}}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}\big|\leq\log 20+\tau_{0},

where the last inequality is due to
\frac{1}{2}\leq 10Ie^{\tau_{0}}
.
Therefore

\displaystyle\big\|\|\log\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{t}\|\big\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\sqrt{D_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}(\log 20+\tau_{0}).

Also,

\displaystyle\max\big\{\|{\mathbb{E}}\log^{2}\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{t}{\bm{X}}_{t}^{\top}\|,\|{\mathbb{E}}\log^{2}\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{t}\|\big\}\leq d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(\log 20+\tau_{0})^{2}.

Using the matrix Bernstein inequality (see e.g. Proposition 2 in
Koltchinskii and Xia 2016
), we have with probability exceeding
1-d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-100}
,

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{t}-{\mathbb{E}}\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T}}+\frac{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T}\bigg)\sqrt{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}(\log 20+\tau_{0}).

Next we consider
\|{\mathbb{E}}\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{1}-{\bm{T}}^{*}\|
:

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{1}-{\bm{T}}^{*}\|

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{1},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle\big){\bm{X}}_{1}\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|{\bm{x}}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\|{\bm{y}}\|_{\ell_{2}}=1}{\bm{x}}^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{1},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle\big){\bm{X}}_{1}{\bm{y}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|{\bm{x}}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\|{\bm{y}}\|_{\ell_{2}}=1}\big[{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log\frac{Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{1},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle\big)^{2}\big]^{1/2}\big[{\mathbb{E}}({\bm{x}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{1}{\bm{y}})^{2}]^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\big[{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log(Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-\log I-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{1},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle\big)^{2}\big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\big[{\mathbb{E}}\big(\log(Y_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-\log\nu_{1}\big)^{2}\big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\exp(\tau_{0}/2)}{\sqrt{I}},

where the last inequality is from Lemma
8
and
\nu_{1}\geq I\exp(-\tau_{0})
.
In conclusion, we have with probability exceeding
1-5Td_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-100}
,

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\frac{Y_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}}{I}{\bm{X}}_{t}-{\bm{T}}^{*}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T}}(\log 20+\tau_{0})+\frac{\exp(\tau_{0}/2)}{\sqrt{I}}.

Next use Lemma 18 in
Shen et al. 2023
, we conclude

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\bm{T}}-{\bm{T}}^{*}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T}}(\log 20+\tau_{0})+\frac{\exp(\tau_{0}/2)}{\sqrt{I}}

under the conditions on intensity and sample size. Since
\textsf{rank}(\widehat{\bm{T}}),\textsf{rank}({\bm{T}}^{*})\leq R
, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\bm{T}}-{\bm{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{Rd_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T}}(\log 20+\tau_{0})+\frac{\sqrt{R}\exp(\tau_{0}/2)}{\sqrt{I}}.

Since
{\mathcal{M}}_{{\mathcal{I}}}
is linear and preserve the norm, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{Rd_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T}}(\log 20+\tau_{0})+\frac{\sqrt{R}\exp(\tau_{0}/2)}{\sqrt{I}}.

Finally using Lemma
1
, we conclude

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{Rd_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T}}(\log 20+\tau_{0})+\frac{\sqrt{R}\exp(\tau_{0}/2)}{\sqrt{I}}\leq c_{m}e^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1},

where the last inequality holds under the intensity and sample size conditions.
∎

G.5
Proof of Theorem
6

In this section we present the proof of
theorem

6
.
We first introduce some notations. For any
\omega\in[d_{1}]\times\cdots\times[d_{m}]
, denote
{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}={\bm{e}}_{\omega_{1}}\otimes\cdots\otimes{\bm{e}}_{\omega_{m}}
be the standard basis for
{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
. Also, recall
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
admits the decomposition
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\cdot({\bm{U}}_{1}^{*},\ldots,{\bm{U}}_{m}^{*})
, and denote
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{t}\cdot({\bm{U}}_{t,1},\ldots,{\bm{U}}_{t,m})
.
Define the event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\forall 0\leq l\leq t,\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+10\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2},

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\lesssim_{m}(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{\frac{l}{2}}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\kappa_{0}^{3}(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}})^{1/2}(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\sigma,

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt|\epsilon_{l}|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}},\hskip 14.22636pt\forall j\in[m],\|{\bm{U}}_{l,j}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}\bigg\},

where
\mu_{0}=20\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu
.
In the following proof, at time step
t
, we are conditioning on the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
. For notation simplicity, we shall drop the subscripts
t
in the expression of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}
, i.e.
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot({\bm{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\bm{U}}_{m})
.
We also introduce the spikiness of a tensor, which is closely related to the incoherence.

Definition 1
.

Let
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
. The spikiness of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
is defined as

\textsf{Spiki}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}):=\frac{\sqrt{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}}{\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\rm{F}}}.

Their relation between spikiness and incoherence is summarized in Lemma
5
. And therefore,
\textsf{Spiki}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})\leq\sqrt{r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\kappa_{0}\cdot\mu_{0}^{m/2}=:\nu_{0}
.

For a tensor
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
of Tucker rank
{\bm{r}}
, we define

\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})=\min_{i=1}^{m}\sigma_{r_{i}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(i)}),\quad\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})=\max_{i=1}^{m}\sigma_{1}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(i)}).

And recall we denote
\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}=\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
and
\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}=\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
.

From
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+10\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}
, we see that

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{2r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}},

(29)

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{2}\lesssim r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\kappa_{0}^{2}
. And

\displaystyle\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l})\geq\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}},

(30)

where the last line holds as long as
\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}
.

We now derive some bounds that will be used frequently.
Recall we write
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot({\bm{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\bm{U}}_{m})
where
{\bm{U}}_{j}
are
\mu_{0}
incoherent under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
. For any
\omega\in[d_{1}]\times\cdots\times[d_{m}]
,

{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}={\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}\times_{j}{\bm{W}}_{j}

with
{\bm{W}}_{j}={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega})_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}
.
Notice these components are mutually orthogonal, using the incoherence of
{\bm{U}}_{j}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}\times_{j}{\bm{W}}_{j}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}}{d^{*}}(\mu_{0}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}/r_{j})\leq(m+1)\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}},

(31)

where the last inequality holds since from the definition of incoherence, we have
\mu_{0}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\leq d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
.
As a result,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(m+1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

(32)

Meanwhile,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\omega}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}\times_{j}{\bm{W}}_{j}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})\leq r^{*}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j}=\overline{\textsf{dof}},

(33)

since

\displaystyle\sum_{\omega}\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}\times_{j}{\bm{W}}_{j}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}^{\perp}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k})^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq d_{j}r_{j}.

Next we bound
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
that is dependent on
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Notice

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}

\displaystyle=({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\times_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}+{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}^{\perp})

\displaystyle=({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\times_{k=1}^{j-1}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}^{*}}\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\times_{k=1}^{j-1}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}^{*}}\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}^{\perp}\times_{k=j+1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}},

where the last inequality holds since
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}^{\perp}=0
and
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}={\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}
. For any
\omega\in[d_{1}]\times\cdots\times[d_{m}]
, using the incoherence of
{\bm{U}}_{j}
and
{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\langle({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}},{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\rangle|=|\langle({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}\rangle|\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}},

and

\displaystyle\quad|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\times_{k=1}^{j-1}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}^{*}}\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}^{\perp}\times_{k=j+1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}},{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\rangle|=|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}^{\perp},{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\times_{k=1}^{j-1}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}^{*}}\times_{k=j+1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}}\rangle|

\displaystyle=|\langle({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}^{\perp},{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\times_{k=1}^{j-1}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}^{*}}\times_{k=j+1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{k}}\rangle|\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\big(\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}}\big).

(34)

On the other hand, using the spikiness of the components of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}
, we can derive a bound that is independent of
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
, and from (
29
),

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}\leq(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}})^{2}\leq 3\frac{\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}}{d^{*}}.

(35)

Recall
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}=(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
and under the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
,
|\epsilon_{t}|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}
. And
\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle^{2}\leq d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}
.
Using (
32
) and (
35
), we can derive a uniform bound for
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
:

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle^{2}+|\epsilon_{t}|^{2})\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lesssim_{m}(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

(36)

We also derive the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
using (
33
):

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d^{*}}\sum_{\omega}d^{*}[{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{\omega}^{2}\cdot d^{*}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2m\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\sigma^{2}.

(37)

Step 1: Bounding
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Using triangle inequality and we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\frac{2}{\eta})\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(38)

We can use Lemma
1
to derive a bound for the first term.
As shown in (
36
), we have

\eta\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim_{m}\eta(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}\mu_{0}^{(m-1)/2}(r^{*})^{1/2}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{1/2}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{1/2}}\leq\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}

as long as
\eta\nu_{0}\mu_{0}^{(m-1)/2}\kappa_{0}(d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*})^{1/2}\lesssim_{m}1
and
\eta\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\mu_{0}^{(m-1)/2}(\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}r^{*}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}\lesssim_{m}1
. We have verified the condition in Lemma
1
, and thus

\displaystyle\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{m}\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}

(39)

for some
C_{m}>0
depending only on
m
.
From (
38
) and (
36
), we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\eta^{3}(\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}+(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

(40)

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}\lesssim_{m}1
and
\eta(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim_{m}1
.
Now we consider
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle+\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{3\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where in the last inequality we use Lemma
4
.
From (
G.5
) and (
G.5
), we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}(2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}_{t}(2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\big(2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\eta)\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+4\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\big(2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+4\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\sigma^{2},

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lesssim_{m}1
. Telescoping this equality and we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+8\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{t-l}\big[2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\big(2\eta^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big)\big]}_{=:D_{l}}.

(41)

Now we use martingale concentration inequality to bound
\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}
. First we consider the uniform bound for
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
using (
G.5
) and (
34
),

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle^{2}+\epsilon_{l}^{2})\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(2d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+2\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\cdot d^{*}\max_{\omega}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

(42)

Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\bigg|\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta\bigg|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{G}}_{l}\rangle-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{G}}_{l}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}\bigg|\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\bigg|.

We consider the uniform bound for
|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{G}}_{l}\rangle|
. Using Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, and (
G.5
),

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{G}}_{l}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}}\sigma\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}).

And therefore the uniform bound for
D_{l}
is as follows

\displaystyle|D_{l}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{t-l}\eta\bigg(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}}\sigma\sqrt{\log d}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{t-l}\eta^{2}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d)\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{t-l}\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1}+\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\sigma^{2}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1},

(43)

where the second inequality uses
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
and
\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
, and the last inequality is from

\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}
and
\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
.

We now consider the variance bound for
D_{l}
. Firstly we consider
{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}
using (
34
) and (
32
),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\leq 4{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle^{4}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+4{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\epsilon_{l}^{4}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\cdot(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{2}\cdot\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\sigma^{4}

\displaystyle=(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{3}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\sigma^{4}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{l}|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|^{2}(\epsilon_{l}^{2}+|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|^{2}+d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\sigma^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}.

So as long as
\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}\frac{r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lesssim_{m}1
,

\displaystyle\text{Var}_{l}D_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{2t-2l}\eta^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\sigma^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\frac{\eta}{2})^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}\sigma^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Together with
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}\text{Var}_{l}D_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{2t+2}\eta\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{3}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\sigma^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{3}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\sigma^{4}+(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\eta\sigma^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{2t+2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\sigma^{4}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1},

where the last inequality holds as long as
\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim\overline{\textsf{dof}}
and
\eta\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
.
Using the variance bound and (
G.5
), we see that with probability exceeding
1-d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\sigma^{2}.

Put this back to (
G.5
), and we see that

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+10\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\sigma^{2}.

(44)

Step 2: Incoherence and entry-wise error bound.

Now we bound the incoherence for
{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}
for
j\in[m]
and the entry-wise bound using the representation formula of spectral projectors. Remember we denote
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot({\bm{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\bm{U}}_{m})
.

For each
j\in[m],i\in[d_{j}]
, we are interested in
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}
and
\|({\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}){\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}
.
Since
{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}
is the top
r_{j}
left singular vectors of
({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})_{(j)}
.
And
{\bm{U}}_{j}
is the left singular vectors of
({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}={\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})^{\top}
.
We can obtain a closed form for
{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}
from Section
D.2
as follows

{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}=\sum_{k\geq 1}{\bm{S}}_{k}.

Here
{\bm{S}}_{k}
depends on
{\bm{U}},{\bm{\Sigma}},{\bm{V}},{\bm{Z}}
defined as follows (see Section
D.2
for more details):
suppose
{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}
admits a compact SVD as
{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}={\bm{Q}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{Q}}_{2}^{\top}
with
{\bm{Q}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{j}\times r_{j}}
and
{\bm{Q}}_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{j}^{-}\times r_{j}}
, and
{\bm{U}}={\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{Q}}_{1}
,
{\bm{V}}=(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l}){\bm{Q}}_{2}
and
{\bm{Z}}=-\eta({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})_{(j)}
.
Here for each
{\bm{S}}_{k}
, it can be written as

{\bm{S}}_{k}=\sum_{{\bm{s}}:s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}),

where
{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}})
takes the following form:

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{1}}{\bm{B}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{2}}{\bm{B}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{3}}\cdots{\bm{B}}_{k}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{k+1}}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{\top},

(45)

where
{\bm{A}}_{1},{\bm{A}}_{2}\in\{{\bm{U}},{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\}
and

{\bm{B}}_{i}\in\{{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}},{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}},{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp},{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\text{ or their transpose}\}.

As a consequence of (
30
),
\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\|=\sigma_{r_{j}}^{-1}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)})\leq 2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}
.
Also,

\displaystyle({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{l}})^{\top}+\sum_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}({\bm{W}}_{l}\otimes_{p\neq j,l}{\bm{U}}_{p})^{\top}+{\bm{W}}_{j}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})^{\top},

(46)

where
{\bm{W}}_{l}={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(l)}(\otimes_{q\neq l}{\bm{U}}_{q}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(l)}^{\dagger},\forall l\in[m]
.

Now we derive the operator norm bound for
\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|,\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|,\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\|,\|{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\|
.
Since
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}=(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
, we see
{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}=0
.
For
\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|
, we have

\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|=|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|\|{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})\|\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}},

where the last inequality holds from the incoherence of
{\bm{U}}_{l}
and
({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}
is of the form
\sqrt{d^{*}}{\bm{e}}_{p}{\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}
.

For
\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|
, we have
{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}={\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})^{\top}{\bm{Q}}_{2}
, and thus

\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|\sqrt{d_{j}r_{j}^{-}\mu_{0}^{m-1}},

where the last inequality holds since
({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}
is of the form
\sqrt{d^{*}}{\bm{e}}_{p}{\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}
and
{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}
is a projector.

For
\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\|
, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{U}}^{\top}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}={\bm{Q}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}\sum_{l\neq j}({\bm{W}}_{l}\otimes_{p\neq j,l}{\bm{U}}_{p})^{\top}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}.

(47)

Notice
{\bm{V}}_{\perp}
is a block matrix of block size
(2^{m-1}-1)\times 1
, where each block takes the form

\otimes_{p\neq j}{\bm{A}}_{p},

where
{\bm{A}}_{p}
takes value either
{\bm{U}}_{p}
or
{\bm{U}}_{p,\perp}
and there exists at least one
p\in[m]\backslash j
,
{\bm{A}}_{p}={\bm{U}}_{p,\perp}
. For each fixed
l\in[m]\backslash j
, the only term that is non-zero is
{\bm{U}}_{l,\perp}\otimes_{p\neq j,l}{\bm{U}}_{p}
.
And each term in (
47
) are mutually orthogonal since the non-zero blocks are at different slots in the block matrix.
With this observation, we see that

\displaystyle\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq j}\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}\bigg(\big(({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(l)}^{\dagger})^{\top}(\otimes_{p\neq l}{\bm{U}}_{p})^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(l)}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{l,\perp}\big)\otimes_{s\neq j,l}{\bm{I}}_{r_{s}}\bigg)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq j}\|{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{l}\big({\bm{U}}_{l,\perp}^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(l)}(\otimes_{p\neq l}{\bm{U}}_{p}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(l)}^{\dagger}\big)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq j}\|{\bm{U}}_{l,\perp}^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(l)}(\otimes_{p\neq l}{\bm{U}}_{p}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(l)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(l)}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\|\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-}}.

(48)

Then we conclude

\displaystyle\max\big\{\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|,\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|,\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\|\big\}\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|\bigg((m-1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\bigg)^{1/2}.

(49)

We also bound
|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t}|
. From (
35
) and under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
, we see

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t}|\lesssim\nu_{0}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

(50)

For different values of
k
, we control
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}
and
{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}
.
Notice that for given
k
and
{\bm{s}}
satisfying
s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k
, if there exists
1\leq j\leq k
,
s_{j}=s_{j+1}=0
, then
{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}})=0
. This holds since
{\bm{B}}_{j}={\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}
or
{\bm{V}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}=0
.
And thus in the following, we only consider
{\bm{s}}
such that
{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}})\neq 0
.

Case 1:
k=1
.
When
k=1
, the closed form is given in (
11
).
Since
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{Z}}=-\eta\big({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\big)_{(j)}
, the expectation of
{\bm{S}}_{1}
is given by

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{1}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}+{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}=\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}+\eta({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*})^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}^{\perp}.

(51)

We compute the conditional expectation

{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}.

Due to symmetry, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle=2\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}=2\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}({\bm{I}}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}){\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle=2\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}-2\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)})({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}-2\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}.

For the first term on the RHS, using the incoherence of
{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}
and
{\bm{U}}_{j}
,

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{j}}{d_{j}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}\kappa_{0}.

For the second term on the RHS, using
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{40}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}+\sqrt{\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\sigma\leq\frac{1}{20}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
and the incoherence of
{\bm{U}}_{j}
,

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)})({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq\frac{1}{10}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}

And
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}={\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}
. So we conclude that (given
\mu_{0}\gtrsim\mu\kappa_{0}^{2}
)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq\frac{2\eta}{5}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}-2\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}.

(52)

Computing
{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}
.
To give an entry-wise bound, we are interested in
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}
.
Notice

\displaystyle\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}=\eta[{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}]({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}+\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}-\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}[{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}]({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}-\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}},

and

\displaystyle\eta({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*})^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}^{\perp}=\eta[({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)})^{\dagger}]^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*})^{\top}+\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}-\eta({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)})^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}-\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}.

Notice the bound of
\|({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)})^{\dagger}\|
can be obtained using Lemma
6
,

\displaystyle\|({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)})^{\dagger}\|\leq 30\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\kappa_{0}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

(53)

Plug in these representations, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle=\big(\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}-\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}\big){\bm{e}}_{i}+\eta[{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}]({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}-\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}[{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}]({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta[({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}_{(j)})^{\dagger}]^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*})^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}-\eta({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(j)}^{*}-({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)})^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle=:\big(\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}-\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}\big){\bm{e}}_{i}+{\bm{v}}.

Using the incoherence of
{\bm{U}}_{j},{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}
and (
53
), we can bound
\|{\bm{v}}\|_{\ell_{2}}
as follows

\displaystyle\|{\bm{v}}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq C\eta\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}.

And as a result,

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}={\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}+\big(\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}-\eta{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}}\big){\bm{e}}_{i}+{\bm{v}},

(54)

where
{\bm{v}}
satisfying
\|{\bm{v}}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq C\eta\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}
.

Case 2:
k=2
.
We discuss according to different
{\bm{s}}
. If
{\bm{s}}=(0,0,2)\text{~or~}(2,0,0)
, then
{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}})=0
. If
{\bm{s}}=(0,1,1)\text{~or~}(1,1,0)
,
then
{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}})={\bm{U}}_{\perp}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}
or its transpose. Together with (
50
), we see

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j}}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{j}r_{j}^{-}}\sigma_{\min}^{-2}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\eta^{2}(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

More specifically, when
{\bm{s}}=(1,1,0)
,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}\|{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}\|\|{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\|

\displaystyle\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{2}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{j}r_{j}^{-})^{1/2}(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-1/2}(d_{j}r_{j}^{-}r^{*})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

When
{\bm{s}}=(0,1,1)
,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{U}}_{\perp}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}\|{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}\|\|{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\|\|{\bm{U}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-1/2}(d_{j}r_{j}^{-}r^{*})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}.

If
{\bm{s}}=(1,0,1)
, then
{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}})={\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}
, and

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{j}r_{j}^{-}\sigma_{\min}^{-2}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\eta^{2}(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2},

and

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}\|{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\|^{2}\|{\bm{U}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{j}r_{j}^{-}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}.

For the case
{\bm{s}}=(0,2,0)
, from the closed form given in (
12
),

{\bm{S}}_{2,1}:={\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}})={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}
.From (
46
) and recall
{\bm{V}}=(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l}){\bm{Q}}_{2}
, we see

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{2}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\sum_{p,q}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p}{\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{e}}_{q}{\bm{e}}_{p}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\textsf{Trace}\bigg(({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}\bigg){\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}

where
{\bm{A}}\leq{\bm{B}}
means
{\bm{B}}-{\bm{A}}
is SPSD and
{\bm{A}}\lesssim{\bm{B}}
means there exists absolute constant
C>0
,
C{\bm{B}}-{\bm{A}}
is SPSD.
And the last inequality holds since
\textsf{Trace}\bigg(({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger\top}\bigg)\leq\|({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}^{\dagger}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lesssim r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}
.
As a result, from the above computation and (
35
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}\lesssim\eta^{2}(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2},

(55)

and

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

Therefore we conclude

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{e}}_{i}={\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}+{\bm{u}},

(56)

fro some
{\bm{u}}
satisfying
\|{\bm{u}}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-1/2}(d_{j}r_{j}^{-}r^{*})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

Case 3:
k\geq 3
and
k
is odd.
For each
{\bm{s}}
such that
{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}})\neq 0
, if at least one of
{\bm{A}}_{1},{\bm{A}}_{2}
takes
{\bm{U}}
, then using (
30
) and (
49
), we see

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{k}(\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j})^{1/2}\bigg((m-1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\bigg)^{k/2}(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{k}.

If both
{\bm{A}}_{1},{\bm{A}}_{2}
take
{\bm{U}}_{\perp}
, there exists at least one
l\in[k]
,
{\bm{B}}_{l}={\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\text{~or~}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}
, for otherwise there exists
l^{\prime}\in[k]
,
s_{l}=s_{l+1}=0
and this term will vanish. Then

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{k}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}\bigg((m-1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\bigg)^{(k-1)/2}(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{k}.

Since there are at most
\binom{2k}{k}\leq 4^{k}
possible
{\bm{s}}
,

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 4^{k}|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{k}(\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j})^{1/2}\bigg((m-1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\bigg)^{k/2}(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{k}.

Notice that from (
50
)

\displaystyle\quad 4^{2}|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{2}(m-1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\eta^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu_{0}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}+\eta^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\leq 1/2,

(57)

where the last inequality holds given
\eta^{2}\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}\lesssim_{m}1
and
(\frac{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}})\gtrsim_{m}\eta^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
.
From (
G.5
), the contribution for such
k
is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 3,k\text{ is odd}}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}\lesssim_{m}(\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j})^{1/2}\eta^{3}\mu_{0}^{3(m-1)/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{3/2}(r^{*})^{3/2}\bigg(\kappa_{0}^{3}\nu_{0}^{3}+\sigma^{3}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-3}\log^{3/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{3/2}\bigg).

(58)

Case 4:
k\geq 4
and
k
is even.
We shall apply (
49
) to bound
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}
:

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{k}\bigg((m-1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\bigg)^{k/2}(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{k}.

Since there are
\binom{2k}{k}\leq 4^{k}
legal
{\bm{s}}
,

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 4^{k}|\eta(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})|^{k}\bigg((m-1)\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\bigg)^{k/2}(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{k}.

From (
G.5
), the contribution for such
k
is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 4,k\text{ is even}}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}\lesssim_{m}\eta^{4}\kappa_{0}^{4}\nu_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{2m-2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}(r^{*})^{2}+\eta^{4}\sigma^{4}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-4}\mu_{0}^{2m-2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}(r^{*})^{2}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}.

(59)

Computing
\sum_{k\geq 3}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}
.
For the entry-wise bound, we can consider simultaneously the cases when
k\geq 3
. In fact, using (
49
),

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim 8^{k}\eta^{k}\bigg(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\bigg)^{k}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-k}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{k/2},

and thus from (
G.5
),

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 3}\|{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\eta^{3}\bigg(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\bigg)^{3}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-3}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{3/2}.

(60)

Step 2.1: Bounding the Frobenius norm error
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
.

Now from (
52
), (
55
), (
58
) and (
59
), we see that

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq(1-2\eta){\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i})+\frac{2\eta}{5}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\underbrace{({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i})+C_{m}\eta^{2}(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}}_{k=2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\underbrace{C_{m}(\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j})^{1/2}\eta^{3}\mu_{0}^{3(m-1)/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{3/2}(r^{*})^{3/2}\bigg(\kappa_{0}^{3}\nu_{0}^{3}+\sigma^{3}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-3}\log^{3/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{3/2}\bigg)}_{k\geq 3,~k\text{ is odd}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\underbrace{C_{m}\eta^{4}\kappa_{0}^{4}\nu_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{2m-2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}(r^{*})^{2}+\eta^{4}\sigma^{4}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-4}\mu_{0}^{2m-2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}(r^{*})^{2}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}_{k\geq 4,~k\text{ is even}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\eta){\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i})+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i})+\frac{\eta}{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}},

where the last inequality holds given
\eta d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}\mu_{0}^{m-1}\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\lesssim_{m}1
and

\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{2}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lesssim_{m}1,\eta\mu_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}m-1}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(r^{*})^{\frac{3}{4}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lesssim_{m}1,\eta\mu_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}m-1}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{\frac{4}{3}}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(r^{*})^{\frac{2}{3}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lesssim_{m}1,

Notice this boils down to
\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{2}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lesssim_{m}1
if
\eta\mu_{0}^{-1}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\leq 1
.

To emphasize the dependence on
t
, we add subscripts for
{\bm{S}}_{1}
and
{\bm{S}}_{2,1}
, namely,
{\bm{S}}_{1,t}
and
{\bm{S}}_{2,1,t}
. Now telescoping this inequality, and we get

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq(1-2\eta)^{t+1}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}},

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-2\eta)^{t-l}\big[({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i})+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i})\big]}_{=:D_{l}}

(61)

Now we use martingale concentration inequality to bound
\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}
. We first consider the uniform bound. Notice

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle-\epsilon_{l}|\cdot|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta(2\nu_{0}\sqrt{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\sqrt{d^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq 8\eta\nu_{0}\kappa_{0}\mu_{0}^{m/2}(r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}r^{*})^{1/2}+C\eta(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

Using
|X-{\mathbb{E}}X|\leq 2B
when
|X|\leq B
, we get

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 16\eta\nu_{0}\kappa_{0}\mu_{0}^{m/2}(r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}r^{*})^{1/2}+C\eta(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

On the other hand, again from (
50
), we have

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle=|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta^{2}(3\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+C\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})4\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}

\displaystyle\leq 24\eta^{2}\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}+C\eta^{2}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

Therefore

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 48\eta^{2}\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}+C\eta^{2}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

As long as
\eta\kappa_{0}\nu_{0}\mu_{0}^{m/2-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(r^{*}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})^{1/2}\lesssim 1
and
\eta(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\mu_{0}^{m/2-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{(r^{*})^{1/2}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
, we have

\displaystyle|D_{l}|\lesssim(1-2\eta)^{t-l}\bigg(\eta\nu_{0}\kappa_{0}\mu_{0}^{m/2}(r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}r^{*})^{1/2}+\eta(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg).

(62)

We also need to bound the variance. In fact,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}D_{l}^{2}\leq 2(1-2\eta)^{2t-2l}[{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}].

And

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}=4\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle^{2}+\epsilon_{l}^{2})({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta^{2}(3\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\sum_{p,q}({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p})^{2}({\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k})^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}},

where in the last inequality we use
\sum_{p\in[d_{j}]}({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p})^{2}\leq 2
and

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{q\in[d_{j}^{-}]}({\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k})^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle=\|(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k})^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\leq(2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{l}\eta^{4}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle+\epsilon_{l})^{4}(|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{4}(\nu_{0}^{4}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{4}+\sigma^{4})d^{*}\sum_{p}({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p})^{4}\|(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k})^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{4}(\nu_{0}^{4}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{4}+\sigma^{4})d^{*}r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-4}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}.

Therefore as long as
\eta\nu_{0}\kappa_{0}\mu_{0}^{\frac{m}{2}-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(r^{*})^{1/2}\lesssim 1
and
\eta(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\mu_{0}^{\frac{m}{2}-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}(\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{1/2}\lesssim 1
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}D_{l}^{2}\lesssim(1-2\eta)^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}.

And the summation has the following upper bound,

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}D_{l}^{2}\lesssim\eta(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}.

(63)

From (
62
) and (
63
), and as a result of Azuma-Bernstein inequality, we see that with probability exceeding
d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-12}
, as long as
\eta\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{d_{j}}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\eta(\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}+\eta\nu_{0}\kappa_{0}\mu_{0}^{m/2}(r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}r^{*})^{1/2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt+\eta(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}(\sigma/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\log^{3/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}},

(64)

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu_{0}^{-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
and
\eta(\sigma^{2}/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2})d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\mu_{0}^{-1}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1

and
\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
.
Now we go back to (
G.5
), and we see as long as
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}.

Taking a union bound over all
i,j
and we see that with probability exceeding
1-md_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-11}
,

\|{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}},\forall j\in[m].

Step 2.2: Bounding the entry-wise error
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
.

To remind the readers that
{\bm{S}}_{1},{\bm{S}}_{2,1},{\bm{w}}
depend on
t
, we add subscripts.
From (
54
), (
56
) and (
60
), we see that

\displaystyle{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}=(1-\eta)\big({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}\big)+{\bm{S}}_{1,t}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{1,t}{\bm{e}}_{i}+{\bm{S}}_{2,1,t}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,t}{\bm{e}}_{i}+{\bm{w}}_{t},

(65)

where
{\bm{w}}_{t}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|{\bm{w}}_{t}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}+\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-1/2}(d_{j}r_{j}^{-}r^{*})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{3}\bigg(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}\bigg)^{3}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-3}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{3/2}.

Now we plug in the bound for
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
, and as long as
\eta\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{2m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{(r^{*})^{2}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lesssim_{m}1
,
\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}/\sigma\gtrsim_{m}\eta^{1/2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}\kappa_{0}^{-2}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}}{(\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
and
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{w}}_{t}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\eta(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}+C\eta\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\sigma.

(66)

Now telescoping (
65
), we see that

\displaystyle{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle=(1-\eta)^{t+1}\big({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{0,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}\big)+\sum_{l=0}^{t}(1-\eta)^{t-l}{\bm{w}}_{l}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-\eta)^{t-l}\bigg({\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}+{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}\bigg)}_{{\bm{f}}_{l}}.

We shall use martingale concentration inequality to bound
\|\sum_{l=0}^{t}{\bm{f}}_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}
. Recall
{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}+{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}
. And therefore

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\eta(d^{*})^{1/2}(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}})^{1/2}\bigg((d^{*})^{1/2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}.
