Using the bound for
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
and we see (given
\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\kappa_{0}^{6}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lesssim\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
)

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\eta(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j})^{1/2}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{l/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}.

On the other hand, recall
{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}={\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}
, and similarly

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\eta^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{l}\kappa_{0}^{4}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

So as long as
\eta^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{l}\kappa_{0}^{4}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\lesssim 1
, we see

\displaystyle\|{\bm{f}}_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\eta(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j})^{1/2}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}

(67)

for all
0\leq l\leq t
. Now we consider the variance bound,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\sum_{p,q}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p}{\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{q}{\bm{e}}_{p}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\sum_{p,q}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{q}{\bm{e}}_{p}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p}{\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(d^{*}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}^{\top}=({\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}+{\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}){\bm{e}}_{i}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}({\bm{U}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}+{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}).

And similar computation as above gives

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}^{\top}\|\lesssim\eta^{2}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

Meanwhile,
{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}
and
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}^{\top}\|
can be bounded as follows

\displaystyle\quad\max\{{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i},\|{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}^{\top}\|\}

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{4}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{2t}\kappa_{0}^{8}\mu_{0}^{2m}(r^{*})^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{4}+\sigma^{4}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}d_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-4}.

Therefore we conclude as long as
\eta^{2}(\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})\mu_{0}^{m-2}d_{j}r_{j}^{-}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\lesssim 1
,

\displaystyle\max\{\|{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{f}}_{l}{\bm{f}}_{l}^{\top}\|,{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{f}}_{l}^{\top}{\bm{f}}_{l}\}\lesssim(1-\eta)^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

And

\displaystyle\max\{\|\sum_{l=0}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{f}}_{l}{\bm{f}}_{l}^{\top}\|,\sum_{l=0}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{f}}_{l}^{\top}{\bm{f}}_{l}\}\lesssim\eta\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

(68)

Then from (
67
) and (
68
), we see with probability exceeding
1-d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-11}
,

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l=0}^{t}{\bm{f}}_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j})^{1/2}\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg[\eta\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg[\eta\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg]^{1/2},

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\mu_{0}^{m-1}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
.
From this and (
66
), we see

\displaystyle\|{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta)^{t+1}\|{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{0,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}+C(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{(t+1)/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\sigma

\displaystyle\quad+C\bigg[\eta\bigg((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t+1}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg)\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\bigg]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta)^{t+1}\|{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{0,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}+C(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{(t+1)/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\sigma,

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta\mu_{0}^{m-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
.
Now we see as long as
{\bm{U}}_{0,j}
is
\mu_{0}
-incoherent, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}^{*}_{j}}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{(t+1)/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}+C\sigma\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}.

(69)

Taking a union bound and with probability exceeding
1-md_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-11}
, (
69
) holds for all
j\in[m]
. Now we are ready to bound
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
. Since

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}

\displaystyle={\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}

\displaystyle=({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\times_{j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t+1},j}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{l<j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{l}^{*}}\times_{j}({\bm{P}}_{U_{t+1,j}}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}})\times_{l>j}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}},

Using triangle inequality and (
69
), we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}})^{1/2}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|{\bm{P}}_{U_{t+1,j}}-{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}}\|_{2,\infty}(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\sqrt{2}(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{(t+1)/2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+C(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\sigma\bigg)(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}[C(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{(t+1)/2}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}+C\sigma\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}})^{1/2}(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}](\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\leq Cm(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{\frac{t+1}{2}}\kappa_{0}^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}})^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+Cm\kappa_{0}^{3}(\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}})^{1/2}(\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\sigma.

Step 3: Controlling the probability.
From Step 1 and Step 2, we have proved under the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
,
{\mathcal{E}}_{t+1}
holds with probability exceeding
1-(1+2md_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-1})d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}\geq 1-3d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
. Then a similar argument as in Step 3 in the proof of Theorem
1
shows

{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{T}^{c})=\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{c})\leq 3Td_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}.

G.6
Proof of Theorem
7

Notice for each
j\in[m]
,
\tilde{\bm{U}}_{j}
is actually the top
r_{j}
left singular vectors of the following matrix:

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}=\frac{1}{T_{1}(T_{1}-1)}\sum_{1\leq i<i^{\prime}\leq T_{1}}Y_{i}Y_{i^{\prime}}({\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}})^{\top}+{\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}).

We denote
{\bm{N}}_{j}={\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})^{\top}
.
Using Wedin’s sin
\Theta
theorem, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}.

Now
\widehat{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}
is a U-statistics of order 2, using standard decoupling techniques for U-statistics (see e.g. Theorem 3.4.1 in
De la Pena and Giné 2012
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(\|\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|\geq t)\leq 15{\mathbb{P}}(\|\bar{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|\geq t),

where

\displaystyle\bar{\bm{N}}_{j}=\frac{1}{T_{1}(T_{1}-1)}\sum_{1\leq i<i^{\prime}\leq T_{1}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i^{\prime}}({\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}})^{\top}+{\mathcal{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i^{\prime}}){\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}),

where
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}=\sqrt{d^{*}}{\bm{\mathcal{E}}}_{\tilde{\omega}_{i}}
, and
\tilde{\omega}_{i},\tilde{Y}_{i}
i.i.d. copy of
\omega_{i},Y_{i}
such that

\displaystyle\tilde{Y}_{i}=\langle\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle+\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}.

For notation simplicity, we drop the subscript
j
, and we denote
m_{1}=d_{j}
,
m_{2}=d_{j}^{-}
,
{\bm{M}}={\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{1}\times m_{2}}
, and
{\bm{X}}_{i}={\mathcal{M}}_{j}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})
,
\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}={\mathcal{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})
.
We define

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1}=\bm{\Delta}_{1}+{\bm{Z}}_{1},\quad{\bm{S}}_{2}=\bm{\Delta}_{2}+{\bm{Z}}_{2},

where

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{1}=\left(\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\langle{\bm{X}}_{i},{\bm{M}}\rangle{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}\right),

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{2}=\left(\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\langle\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i},{\bm{M}}\rangle\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}\right),

\displaystyle{\bm{Z}}_{1}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\epsilon_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i},

\displaystyle{\bm{Z}}_{2}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}.

Recall we write
{\bm{N}}_{j}={\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}
and thus

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{T_{1}}{2(T_{1}-1)}({\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}+{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top})+\frac{T_{1}}{2(T_{1}-1)}({\bm{S}}_{1}+{\bm{S}}_{2}){\bm{M}}^{\top}+\frac{T_{1}}{2(T_{1}-1)}{\bm{M}}({\bm{S}}_{1}+{\bm{S}}_{2})^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{T_{1}-1}\left(\frac{1}{2T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i}({\bm{X}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}+\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top})-{\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\right).

We denote
M=\max\{m_{1},m_{2}\}
.
Using matrix Bernstein inequality (see e.g.
Koltchinskii et al. 2011
) and Lemma 2.1 in
Koltchinskii and Xia 2016
, we have the following event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{1}=\bigg\{\max\{\|{\bm{Z}}_{1}\|,\|{\bm{Z}}_{2}\|\}\leq C\sigma\frac{\sqrt{M\log M}}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\bigg\}

holds with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
as long as
T_{1}\gtrsim\min\{m_{1},m_{2}\}\log^{2}M
. And from matrix Bernstein inequality,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{2}=\bigg\{\max\{\|\bm{\Delta}_{1}\|,\|\bm{\Delta}_{2}\|\}\leq\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\frac{\sqrt{Md^{*}\log M}}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\bigg\}

holds with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
as long as
T_{1}\gtrsim\min\{m_{1},m_{2}\}\log M
.
Moreover, we consider the event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{3}=\bigg\{\|\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{t=1}Y_{t}{\bm{X}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{1,\infty},

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{t=1}\tilde{Y}_{t}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{1,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{d^{*}}\big(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}M\big)(\frac{1}{m_{2}}+\frac{\log M}{T_{1}})\bigg\},

where
\|{\bm{S}}^{\top}\|_{1,\infty}=\max_{i\in[m_{2}]}\|{\bm{S}}{\bm{e}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}
for
{\bm{S}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{1}\times m_{2}}
. From Chernoff bound (see e.g. proof of Theorem 2 in
Yuan and Zhang 2017
), we see
{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{3})\geq 1-T_{1}M^{-10}
.
We now proceed our proof conditioning on
{\mathcal{E}}_{1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{2}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{3}
.

Upper bound for
\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}\|,\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|
.

We only consider the upper bound for
\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|
. Notice
{\bm{S}}_{2}
is independent of
Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}
. We shall proceed conditioning on
{\bm{S}}_{2}
. In fact,

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}(Y_{i}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{M}}^{\top}),

and that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\bm{S}}_{2}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}\preccurlyeq(d^{*}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})m_{1}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\preccurlyeq(d^{*}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\textsf{Tr}({\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}){\bm{I}}_{m_{1}}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\quad\bigg\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{m_{1}}\|{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{1,\infty}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)+\|{\bm{M}}\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}\|.

Now using the matrix Bernstein inequality (see e.g. Proposition 2 in
Koltchinskii and Xia 2016
), we have with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
, the following event holds

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{4}=\bigg\{\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log^{3}M}}{T_{1}}\bigg(\sqrt{m_{1}}\|{\bm{S}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{1,\infty}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)+\|{\bm{M}}\|\|{\bm{S}}_{2}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)\|{\bm{S}}_{2}\|\sqrt{\frac{m_{1}\log M}{T_{1}}}\bigg\}.

And thus under
{\mathcal{E}}_{1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{2}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{3}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{4}
, as long as
T_{1}\gtrsim\sqrt{\min\{m_{1},m_{2}\}}\log^{2}M
,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{\top}\|\lesssim(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)^{2}\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}M}\log M}{T_{1}}.

Upper bound for
\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|,\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|
.

Notice

\displaystyle{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}.

And it is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\quad\max\bigg\{\big\|{\mathbb{E}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})\big\|,\big\|{\mathbb{E}}{\bm{M}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}\big\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim m_{1}(\sigma^{2}+d^{*}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2})\|{\bm{M}}\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle\quad\bigg\|\|(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\sqrt{m_{1}d^{*}}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}.

Using matrix Bernstein inequality again, and we see with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
, the following event holds,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{5}=\bigg\{\|{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|,\|{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}d^{*}}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\sqrt{\log M}}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\bigg\}.

Upper bound for
\frac{1}{T_{1}-1}\left(\frac{1}{2T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i}({\bm{X}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}+\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top})-{\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}\right)
.

In fact, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}\tilde{Y}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})+\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}(Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{M}}^{\top}.

Here the second term is
{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{M}}^{\top}
, which is just bounded above. Now we conditioned on
Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i},i=1,\cdots,T_{1}
. One can similarly show

\displaystyle\bigg\|\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{d^{*}}(\sigma+\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}})\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\|.

And

\displaystyle\quad\max\bigg\{\big\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})^{\top}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\big\|,\big\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}}){\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}-{\bm{M}})^{\top}\big\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim m_{1}(\sigma^{2}+d^{*}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2})\max\bigg\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\right\|,\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{i}\right\|\bigg\}.

And we have from matrix Bernstein inequality again, and we see with probability exceeding
1-M^{-10}
, the following event holds,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{6}=\bigg\{

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\lesssim\sqrt{d^{*}}(\sigma+\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}})\frac{\log M}{T_{1}}\max_{i}\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{m_{1}}(\sigma+\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}})\frac{\sqrt{\log M}}{T_{1}}\sqrt{\max\bigg\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}\right\|,\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{i}\right\|\bigg\}}\bigg\}.

Now we consider the following event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{7}=\bigg\{\max_{i}\|Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}\|\lesssim(\sigma\sqrt{\log M}+\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}})\bigg\},

which holds with probability exceeding
1-T_{1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
.
So we conclude on
{\mathcal{E}}_{6}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{7}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{1}}Y_{i}{\bm{X}}_{i}(\tilde{Y}_{i}\tilde{\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{M}}^{\top})\|\lesssim(\sigma+\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}})^{2}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{d^{*}\log^{3}M}}{T_{1}}+\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}}\log M}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\bigg).

Finalize the proof for subspace.

Under
\bigcap_{k=1}^{7}{\mathcal{E}}_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)^{2}\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}M}\log M}{T_{1}}+\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}d^{*}}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\sqrt{\log M}}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{T_{1}}(\sigma+\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}})^{2}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{d^{*}\log^{3}M}}{T_{1}}+\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}}\log M}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)^{2}\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}M}\log M}{T_{1}}+\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}d^{*}}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\sqrt{\log M}}{\sqrt{T_{1}}}.

We now plug in the
m_{1},m_{2},M
and we obtain

\displaystyle\|\bar{\bm{N}}_{j}-{\bm{N}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)^{2}\frac{\sqrt{d_{j}^{2}+d^{*}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T_{1}}+\frac{\sqrt{d_{j}d^{*}}(\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\sigma)\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}}{\sqrt{T_{1}}},

where
\lesssim_{m}
hides constant depending only on the dimension
m
of the tensor. Finally, notice
\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{M}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\mu^{m/2}\sqrt{r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
, we conclude with probability exceeding
1-4T_{1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\bm{U}}_{j}\tilde{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\mu^{m}r^{*}\kappa_{0}^{2}+\sigma^{2}/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2})\frac{(d_{j}+\sqrt{d^{*}})\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{d_{j}}(\mu^{m/2}\sqrt{r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\sigma)\mu^{m/2}\sqrt{r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}\sqrt{T_{1}}}.

Finally from Remark 6.2 in
Keshavan et al. 2010
, we conclude

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\mu^{m}r^{*}\kappa_{0}^{2}+\sigma^{2}/\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2})\frac{(d_{j}+\sqrt{d^{*}})\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{T_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{d_{j}}(\mu^{m/2}\sqrt{r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+\sigma)\mu^{m/2}\sqrt{r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sqrt{\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}\sqrt{T_{1}}}.

and
\textsf{Incoh}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j})\leq 3\mu
.

Core tensor estimation.

Now we consider the accuracy for the core tensor estimation.
For notation simplicity, we shall use
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
instead of
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t-T_{1}}
and then
\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j},j\in[m]
is independent of
\{{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T_{2}}
. We denote the loss function

\displaystyle L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})=\frac{1}{2T_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{2}}\big(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{1}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{1}\cdots\times_{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{m}\rangle-Y_{t}\big)^{2}.

And simple computation shows
\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})=\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{2}}\big(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\rangle-Y_{t}\big){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j=1}^{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}
, and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}})={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}).

It would also be helpful to notice
\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})=0
since
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}
is the least square estimator.
We now decompose

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle=\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})-{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})-\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})\rangle}_{=:\beta_{1}}-\underbrace{\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\rangle}_{\beta_{2}}.

Using the expression for
{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{C}})
and
\nabla L(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}})
above, we can further decompose
\beta_{1}
as

\displaystyle\beta_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle+\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\epsilon_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle.

And we have

\displaystyle\beta_{1,1}

\displaystyle:=\bigg|-\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt+\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle=|\langle\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}{\bm{g}}_{i}{\bm{g}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{I}},{\bm{a}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\rangle|,

where
{\bm{g}}_{i}=\textsf{Vec}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r^{*}}
satisfies
\|\langle{\bm{g}}_{i},{\bm{m}}\rangle\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq C_{0}\|{\bm{m}}\|_{\ell_{2}}
(notice we here implicitly use the fact
\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}
and
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}
are independent), and

{\bm{a}}=\textsf{Vec}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}),{\bm{b}}=\textsf{Vec}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\big).

Using matrix Bernstein inequality, and we see with probability exceeding
1-e^{-\delta_{1}}
(for some
\delta_{1}\leq T_{2}
to be specified),

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}{\bm{g}}_{i}{\bm{g}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{I}}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}{\bm{g}}_{i}{\bm{g}}_{i}^{\top}-{\bm{I}},{\bm{a}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}\|{\bm{a}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\|_{*}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}.

We have

\displaystyle\quad\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})=\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}+{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})

\displaystyle=\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\bm{I}}_{r_{1}}\cdots\times_{j}({\bm{I}}_{r_{j}}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\times_{j+1}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{*})\cdots\times_{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{m}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{m}^{*}).

(70)

Therefore

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|{\bm{I}}_{r_{j}}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}.

So we conclude

\displaystyle\beta_{1,1}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\bigg(\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}\bigg).

On the other hand, using matrix Bernstein inequality, we conclude with probability exceeding
1-e^{-\delta_{1}}
,

\displaystyle\beta_{1,2}

\displaystyle:=\bigg|\frac{1}{T_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{2}}\epsilon_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\times_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top},\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\rangle\bigg|\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}\sigma\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

For
\beta_{2}
, using once again the decomposition in (
G.6
),

\displaystyle|\beta_{2}|

\displaystyle=|\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\mathbb{E}}\nabla L({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\rangle=\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\rangle|

\displaystyle=\big|\sum_{j=1}^{m}\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\bm{I}}_{r_{1}}\cdots\times_{j}({\bm{I}}_{r_{j}}-\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*})\times_{j+1}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j+1}^{*})\cdots\times_{m}(\widehat{\bm{U}}_{m}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{m}^{*})\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Putting everything together and we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\bigg(\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}+\sigma\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}.

As a result, as long as
\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}\leq\frac{1}{2}
we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}(\log r^{*}+\delta_{1})}{T_{2}}}\sigma

Finally, we set
\delta_{1}=\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
, and we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j=1}^{m}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}\widehat{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{m}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}

under the given sample size condition and SNR condition.

G.7
Proof of Theorem
8

The proof of this theorem is similar with the proof of Theorem
6
. We shall frequently use the results therein.
Notice from Assumption
5
, we see that
\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\alpha/2
.
At time
t
, we shall condition on the following event,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t}=\bigg\{\forall 0\leq l\leq t,\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\frac{\eta L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}{\gamma_{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\forall j\in[m],\|{\bm{U}}_{l,j}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}\bigg\}.

for some absolute constant
C>0
and
\mu_{0}=20\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu
. From Lemma
5
, we see that
\textsf{Spiki}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})\leq(\frac{r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}})^{1/2}\mu_{0}^{m/2}\kappa_{0}=:\nu_{0}
.
Then from Assumption
5
and under the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
, we see that

\sqrt{d^{*}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\sqrt{d^{*}}\frac{\nu_{0}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\leq\nu_{0}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}})\leq\nu_{0}\bigg(2\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+C\sqrt{\frac{\eta L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\bigg)+\frac{\alpha}{2}\leq\alpha,

as long as
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\nu_{0}}
and
\sqrt{\frac{\eta L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\lesssim\alpha
.

Recall the loss function is
\ell({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},\mathfrak{D}_{t})=h(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})
and thus the gradient
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}
is given by
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}=h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
.

Step 1: Relation between
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
and
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.

We first bound
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Recall
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}=h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
and
|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|\leq L_{\alpha}
since
|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq\alpha
and for
y\in\{0,1\}
,
\sup_{|\theta|\leq\alpha}|h_{\theta}(\theta,y)|\leq L_{\alpha}
from the definition of
L_{\alpha}
.
Moreover,
{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
has the same form as in the tensor completion case, so from (
32
),

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq L_{\alpha}(m+1)^{1/2}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{1/2}.

So as long as
\eta L_{\alpha}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{1/2}\lesssim_{m}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
,
\eta\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}{8}
. Since we have verified the condition of Lemma
1
, we see

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\frac{2}{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}})\|{\mathcal{R}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\frac{\eta^{3}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}}{\gamma_{\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}+(1+\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}+(1+\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

(71)

as long as
\eta L_{\alpha}^{2}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{2}\frac{1}{\gamma_{\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\lesssim_{m}1
.

Denote
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}=\nabla_{{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}}\ell({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},\mathfrak{D}_{t})=h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle,Y_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
. Then
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}\cdot\mathds{1}({\mathcal{E}}_{t})=0
, and thus

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}\rangle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*})\rangle

Recall from the definition of
\gamma_{\alpha},\mu_{\alpha}
,
h(\theta,Y_{t})
is
\gamma_{\alpha}
-strongly convex and
h_{\theta}(\theta,y)
is
\mu_{\alpha}
-Lipschitz w.r.t.
|\theta|\leq\alpha
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle\cdot\left(h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})-h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle),Y_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\gamma_{\alpha}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle^{2}=\gamma_{\alpha}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t}|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\cdot|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})-h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle,Y_{t})|

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{\alpha}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}|\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\cdot|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C\mu_{\alpha}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C_{m}\mu_{\alpha}\frac{\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}},

where the last inequality is from Lemma
4
. So as long as
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\gamma_{\alpha}}{\mu_{\alpha}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
and
\sqrt{\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\gamma_{\alpha}}{\mu_{\alpha}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}
, we see

{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\geq 0.5\gamma_{\alpha}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Also from (
33
), we see

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}={\mathbb{E}}_{t}|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq L_{\alpha}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-2\eta\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-2\eta\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}).

Together with (
G.7
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta(1+\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{2})\bigg({\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\rangle\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}(1+\frac{\eta\gamma_{\alpha}}{2})(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}).

Telescoping this inequality and we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\frac{\eta L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t-l}2\eta(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})\bigg({\mathbb{E}}_{l}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle\bigg)}_{=:D_{l}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t-l}\eta^{2}(1+\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})(\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})}_{=:F_{l}}.

Now we first use martingale concentration inequality to bound
\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}
. In fact, from (
32
)

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C_{m}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\bigg[(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{\frac{l}{2}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+(\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\bigg]L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}}.

And thus

\displaystyle|D_{l}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\eta\bigg[(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{\frac{t}{2}}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+(\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}\bigg]L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1}+C_{m}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+C_{m}\eta^{3/2}(\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1}+C_{m}\eta^{3/2}(\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{1/2}L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}},

as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\log^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim_{m}1
.
On the other hand,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\rangle^{2}\leq L_{\alpha}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle^{2}\leq L_{\alpha}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}\text{Var}_{l}D_{l}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{l=0}^{t}(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{l=0}^{t}(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\bigg(2(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{l}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta^{2}\frac{L_{\alpha}^{4}}{\gamma_{\alpha}^{2}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{2t+2}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}(\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{-1}+C\eta^{2}\frac{L_{\alpha}^{4}}{\gamma_{\alpha}^{2}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

As a result, with probability exceeding
1-2d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
, as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
,

\displaystyle|\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}|

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}.

Now we consider
\sum_{l=0}^{t}F_{l}
. From (
32
),

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(m+1)L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

And thus

\displaystyle|F_{l}|\lesssim_{m}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

On the other hand,

{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\lesssim_{m}L_{\alpha}^{4}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

And

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}\text{Var}_{l}F_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\sum_{l=0}^{t}(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{2t-2l}\eta^{4}L_{\alpha}^{4}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\eta^{3}L_{\alpha}^{4}(\gamma_{\alpha})^{-1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}.

So as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
, with probability exceeding
1-2d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle|\sum_{l=0}^{t}F_{l}|\lesssim_{m}\eta^{3/2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1/2}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{1/2}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

These conclude under event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
, with probability exceeding
1-4d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{m}\eta^{3/2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1/2}(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{1/2}\log^{1/2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\frac{1}{4}\eta\gamma_{\alpha})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}L_{\alpha}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}},

as long as
\eta\gamma_{\alpha}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
.

Step 2: Incoherence of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}
.
The proof of this part is mostly similar with the proof in Step 2 of Theorem
6
. We will use the same notation and point out the differences. Recall we write
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}={\bm{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot({\bm{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\bm{U}}_{m})
.

Now for each
j\in[m],i\in[d_{j}]
, we consider
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}
. Since
{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}
is the top
r_{j}
left singular vectors of
({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})_{(j)}
.
And
{\bm{U}}_{j}
is the left singular vectors of
({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})_{(j)}={\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})^{\top}
.
We can obtain a closed form for
{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}
from Section
D.2
as follows

{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}-{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}=\sum_{k\geq 1}{\bm{S}}_{k}.

Here
{\bm{S}}_{k}
depends on
{\bm{U}},{\bm{\Sigma}},{\bm{V}},{\bm{Z}}
defined as follows (see Section
D.2
for more details):
suppose
{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}
admits a compact SVD as
{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}={\bm{Q}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{Q}}_{2}^{\top}
with
{\bm{Q}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{j}\times r_{j}}
and
{\bm{Q}}_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{j}^{-}\times r_{j}}
, and
{\bm{U}}={\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{Q}}_{1}
,
{\bm{V}}=(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l}){\bm{Q}}_{2}
and
{\bm{Z}}=-\eta({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})_{(j)}
.
Here for each
{\bm{S}}_{k}
, it can be written as

{\bm{S}}_{k}=\sum_{{\bm{s}}:s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}),

where
{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}})
takes the following form:

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{1}}{\bm{B}}_{1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{2}}{\bm{B}}_{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{3}}\cdots{\bm{B}}_{k}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-s_{k+1}}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{\top},

(72)

where
{\bm{A}}_{1},{\bm{A}}_{2}\in\{{\bm{U}},{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\}
and

{\bm{B}}_{i}\in\{{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}},{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}},{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp},{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\text{ or their transpose}\}.

Under the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
, as long as
\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\lesssim\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}
, we have
\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\|\geq 2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}
.
Since now
{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}=h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}
, and
|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|\leq L_{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|=\eta|h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})|\cdot\|{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})\|\leq\eta L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}},

\displaystyle\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}_{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\|\leq\eta L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{j}r_{j}^{-}},\quad\|{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}_{\perp}\|\leq\eta L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-}}.

Now we bound
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}
.

Case 1:
k=1
.
Recall
{\bm{Z}}=-\eta({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t})_{(j)}=-\eta h_{\theta}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle,Y_{t})({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}
. Unfortunately, we are unable to write the expectation
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{Z}}
as before. As a remedy, we notice for the logistic link
f(\theta)=(1+e^{-\theta})^{-1}
,

\displaystyle h_{\theta}(\theta,Y_{t})=-Y_{t}\frac{f^{\prime}(\theta)}{f(\theta)}+(1-Y_{t})\frac{f^{\prime}(\theta)}{1-f(\theta)}=(f(\theta)-Y_{t})\frac{f^{\prime}(\theta)}{f(\theta)(1-f(\theta))}=f(\theta)-Y_{t}.

Also notice that
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{t}}(f(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle)-Y_{t}){\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}=0
since
Y_{t}\sim\text{Ber}(f(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle))
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{Z}}

\displaystyle=-\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}^{*})_{(j)}=-\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\bigg(f(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle)-f(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle)\bigg)({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}

\displaystyle=-\eta(\frac{1}{4}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\rangle-\frac{1}{4}\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle+\zeta)({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)},

where
\zeta
is some number satisfying
|\zeta|\leq\frac{1}{12\sqrt{3}}d^{*}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}+\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2})\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}
as long as
\eta\frac{L_{\alpha}^{2}}{\gamma_{\alpha}}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\leq\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.
Now we are able to consider the following expectation,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}=-\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}\bigg({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{(j)}+{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\zeta({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}\bigg){\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}.

We consider
-\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\zeta({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}
, using (
46
),

\displaystyle|\eta{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\zeta({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\eta\nu_{0}^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}\sqrt{d^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}}2\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}\mu_{0}^{m/2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\sqrt{r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}.

Now as long as
\nu_{0}^{2}\kappa_{0}\mu_{0}^{m/2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\sqrt{r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}
, we have similarly with (
52
),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq\frac{\eta}{5}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}-\frac{\eta}{2}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{j}{\bm{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}.

(73)

Case 2:
k=2
.
We discuss according to different
{\bm{s}}
. If
{\bm{s}}=(0,0,2)\text{~or~}(2,0,0)
, then
{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}})=0
. If
{\bm{s}}=(1,1,0)\text{~or~}(0,1,1)
, then
{\bm{B}}_{1}
or
{\bm{B}}_{2}={\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}
or its transpose, as a result

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j}}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-}}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\sqrt{\frac{r_{j}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}d_{j}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

If
{\bm{s}}=(1,0,1)
, then

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})\lesssim_{m}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}\frac{r_{j}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}d_{j}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

For the case
{\bm{s}}=(0,2,0)
, the closed form is given in (
12
),
we keep it and compute its conditional expectation

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}={\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger})^{\top}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{(j)}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\cdot\sum_{p\in[d_{j}],q\in[d_{j}^{-}]}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p}|^{2}\|{\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{l\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{l})^{\top}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

(74)

Case 3:
k\geq 3
and
k
is odd.
For each legal
{\bm{s}}
, if at least one of
{\bm{A}}_{1},{\bm{A}}_{2}
takes
{\bm{U}}
, then

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq\sqrt{\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j}}(\eta L_{\alpha})^{k}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-})^{k/2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-k}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}).

If both
{\bm{A}}_{1},{\bm{A}}_{2}
take
{\bm{U}}_{\perp}
, there exists at least one
l\in[k]
,
{\bm{B}}_{l}={\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}\text{~or~}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{U}}
. Then

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq(\eta L_{\alpha})^{k}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-})^{(k-1)/2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-k}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}).

Since there are at most
\binom{2k}{k}\leq 4^{k}
legal
{\bm{s}}
,

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 4^{k}\sqrt{\mu_{0}r_{j}/d_{j}}(\eta L_{\alpha})^{k}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-})^{k/2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-k}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}).

Now as long as
\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\lesssim_{m}1
, we have

4^{2}(\eta L_{\alpha})^{2}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-})\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t})\leq 1/2
.
Therefore the contribution for such
k
is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 3,k\text{ is odd}}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m}(\eta L_{\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{3}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}.

(75)

Case 4:
k\geq 4
and
k
is even.
We shall apply trivial bound to
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}
.

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}({\bm{s}}){\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq(\eta L_{\alpha})^{k}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-})^{k/2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-k}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}).

Since there are
\binom{2k}{k}\leq 4^{k}
legal
{\bm{s}}
,

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 4^{k}(\eta L_{\alpha})^{k}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}\sum_{l\neq j}d_{l}r_{l}^{-})^{k/2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-k}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}).

And the contribution for such
k
is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 4,k\text{ is even}}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{k}{\bm{e}}_{i}\lesssim_{m}(\eta L_{\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{4}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}.

(76)

Now from (
73
) - (
76
), we see if
d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}/d_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\leq C,r_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}/r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\leq C
,

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta){\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i})+\frac{\eta}{5}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{C_{m}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m}\sqrt{\frac{r_{j}}{d_{j}}r^{*}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}+C_{m}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\mu_{0}^{m}\frac{r_{j}}{d_{j}}\frac{d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}}{r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i})}_{k=2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{C_{m}(\eta L_{\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{3}(\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*})^{1/2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}}_{k\geq 3\text{ and }k\text{ is odd}}+\underbrace{C_{m}(\eta L_{\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{4}(\mu_{0}^{m-1}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1})^{2}}_{k\geq 4\text{ and }k\text{ is even}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta){\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}{\bm{U}}_{t,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}+\frac{2\eta}{5}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1}{\bm{e}}_{i})+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1}{\bm{e}}_{i}),

as long as
\eta d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r_{j}^{-}(\frac{L_{\alpha}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}\lesssim_{m}1
,
\eta^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}(r^{*})^{3/2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}(\frac{L_{\alpha}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{3}\mu_{0}^{3m/2-2}\lesssim_{m}1
,
\eta^{3}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{3}(r^{*})^{2}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}(\frac{L_{\alpha}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{4}\mu_{0}^{2m-3}\lesssim_{m}1
.

Now telescoping this inequality and we get

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta)^{t+1}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}+\frac{4}{5}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=0}^{t}\underbrace{(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta)^{t-l}\big[({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i})+({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{t}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i})\big]}_{=:D_{l}}

(77)

Now we use martingale concentration inequality to bound
|\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}|
. We first consider the uniform bound. Notice

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta L_{\alpha}\cdot|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta L_{\alpha}\sqrt{d^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l})\leq 4\eta L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}.

Using
|X-{\mathbb{E}}X|\leq 2B
when
|X|\leq B
, we get

|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 8\eta L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|

\displaystyle=|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\sigma_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l})\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}.

Therefore
|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}_{l}{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|\leq 8\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}.

So we have the uniform bound for
D_{l}
as follows,

\displaystyle|D_{l}|\leq 8\eta L_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-1}+8\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}d_{j}.

(78)

We also need to bound the variance. In fact,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}D_{l}^{2}\leq 2(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta)^{2t-2l}[{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}].

And

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle=4\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{p,q}({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p})^{2}({\bm{e}}_{q}^{\top}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k})^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d_{j}}.

Also,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2,1,l}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{l}|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\eta^{4}L_{\alpha}^{4}(|{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{V}}^{\top}({\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})_{(j)}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{i}|^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{4}L_{\alpha}^{4}d^{*}\sum_{p}({\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{P}}_{{\bm{U}}}^{\perp}{\bm{e}}_{p})^{4}\|(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k}){\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}^{\dagger}{\bm{\mathcal{C}}}_{(j)}(\otimes_{k\neq j}{\bm{U}}_{k})^{\top}{\bm{V}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{4}L_{\alpha}^{4}d^{*}r_{j}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-4}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m-1}r_{j}^{-}}{d_{j}^{-}}.

Therefore as long as
\eta^{2}d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\mu_{0}^{-1}\lesssim 1
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}D_{l}^{2}\lesssim(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta)^{2t-2l}\eta^{2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d_{j}}.

And

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}D_{l}^{2}\lesssim\eta L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d_{j}}.

(79)

From (
78
) and (
79
), and as a result of Azuma-Bernstein inequality, we see that with probability exceeding
d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-12}
, as long as
\eta d_{j}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
,
\eta^{2}(\frac{L_{\alpha}}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}})^{2}\mu_{0}^{m-2}d_{j}r_{j}^{-}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}
,

\displaystyle|\sum_{l=0}^{t}D_{l}|\lesssim\sqrt{\eta L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{m}r^{*}}{d_{j}}\log d}\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}},

(80)

where the last inequality holds as long as
\eta d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}r^{*}r_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}L_{\alpha}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\mu_{0}^{m-2}\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}\lesssim 1
. Now we go back to (
G.7
), and we see as long as
{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}{\bm{U}}_{0,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq\frac{1}{10}\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{e}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}^{\top}{\bm{e}}_{i}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}}.

Taking a union bound over all
i,j
and we see that with probability exceeding
1-md_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-11}
,

\|{\bm{U}}_{t+1,j}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r_{j}}{d_{j}},\forall j\in[m].

Step 3: Controlling the probability.
From Step 1 and Step 2, we have proved under the event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
,
{\mathcal{E}}_{t+1}
holds with probability exceeding
1-(4+md_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-1})d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}\geq 1-5d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{-10}
. Then a similar argument as in Step 3 in the proof of Theorem
1
shows

{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{T}^{c})=\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}\cap{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{c})\leq 5Td^{-10}.

G.8
Proof of Theorem
9

Using Theorem 1 in
Davenport et al. 2014
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d^{*}}\|\widehat{\bm{M}}-{\bm{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{1,\alpha}\frac{1}{d^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{R(D_{1}+D_{2})}{T}}\sqrt{1+\frac{(D_{1}+D_{2})\log(D_{1}D_{2})}{T}},

where
C_{1,\alpha}=C\alpha L_{\alpha}\gamma_{\alpha}^{-1}
. And from Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C_{1,\alpha}^{1/2}\left(\frac{R(D_{1}+D_{2})}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left(1+\frac{(D_{1}+D_{2})\log(D_{1}D_{2})}{T}\right)^{1/4}\leq c_{m}\gamma_{\alpha}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}

under the given condition.

G.9
Proof of Theorem
10

Proof.

The proof is mostly the same as the ones in Theorem
1
and Theorem
6
.
We shall consider online tensor linear regression. The proof for online tensor completion is similar and is thus omitted.

Regret bound for online tensor linear regression.

At time
t
, we denote the event

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}=\bigg\{\forall 0\leq l\leq t,\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log T)^{1/2},|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\rangle|\leq C\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T,

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T,\quad|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T,

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{l},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T\bigg\},

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C(\overline{\textsf{dof}}\cdot\log T)^{1/2},|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\rangle|\leq C\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T,

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T,\quad|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T,

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt|\langle{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle|\leq C\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\log^{1/2}T\bigg\}.

Notice these events are slightly different with the one defined in the proof of Theorem
1
in that we replace
\log d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}
with
\log T
. Notice under the assumption
T\leq d_{{\textsf{\tiny max}}}^{C_{0}m}
for some large but absolute constant
C_{0}>0
, the proof of Theorem
1
is still valid.
Therefore,
{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t})\geq 1-14tT^{-10}\geq 1-14T^{-9}
from the previous proof.
Notice
\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\subset\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}
.
Moreover, using law of total probability,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c}|\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}){\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c}|\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}){\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{c}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1})+{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq 14T^{-10}+14T^{-9}\leq 15T^{-9}.

where the last inequality holds from Step 3 in the proof of Theorem
1
.

We split
{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
into two terms,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t})}_{\textsf{A}_{t}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{c})}_{\textsf{B}_{t}}.

(81)

Controlling
{\textsf{A}}_{t}
.

Notice
{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t})={\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t-1}\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}){\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})
.
We first control
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})
. Using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we obtain,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\eta^{-1})\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\eta)\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{t}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})^{4}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\big({\mathbb{E}}_{t}(\langle{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\rangle-\epsilon_{t})^{8}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{8}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+\sigma^{4})\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}.

(82)

Under
\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}
, we can apply Lemma
1
, and we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})}{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\eta^{4}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+\sigma^{4})\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\eta^{4}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}+C_{m}\eta^{4}\sigma^{4}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}.

From (
25
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})\geq\frac{3}{4}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And from Lemma
3
, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})\leq(3\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2})\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

These together give,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})
