\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t},{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\rangle\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{3}{2}\eta+3\eta^{2})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\sigma^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

Therefore for
\eta
that satisfies the conditions in Theorem
2
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\eta^{3}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}+C_{m}\eta^{3}\sigma^{4}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{-2}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta)\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\eta^{2}\sigma^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{4})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+3\eta^{2}\sigma^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

(83)

Notice this is valid for
t\geq 0
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\textsf{A}}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{4}){\textsf{A}}_{t-1}+3\eta^{2}\sigma^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+12\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

(84)

Therefore

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\textsf{A}}_{t}\leq C(\eta^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta T\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}).

(85)

Controlling
{\textsf{B}}_{t}
.

Since
\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}=\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}
, we have
\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{c}=\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c}
. And therefore,

\displaystyle\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{c})=\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})+\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})+\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c}).

So we can further split
\textsf{B}_{t}
into

\displaystyle\textsf{B}_{t}=\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})}_{\textsf{B}_{t,1}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})}_{\textsf{B}_{t,2}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}\cap\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})}_{\textsf{B}_{t,3}}.

From the tower property and
\mathds{1}({\mathcal{F}}_{1}\cap{\mathcal{F}}_{2})=\mathds{1}({\mathcal{F}}_{1})\mathds{1}({\mathcal{F}}_{2})
for any events
{\mathcal{F}}_{1},{\mathcal{F}}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t,1}={\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t-1}\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}){\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}).

From (
G.9
), we have

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t,1}\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{4}){\textsf{B}}_{t-1}+3\eta^{2}\sigma^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}.

(86)

For
{\textsf{B}}_{t,2},{\textsf{B}}_{t,3}
, we have

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t,2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t-1}({\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4})^{1/2}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}^{1/2}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}),

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t,3}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t-1}({\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4})^{1/2}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}^{1/2}(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}^{c})\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t-1}^{c}).

This motivates us to consider the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}
.
We shall use the bound for
\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}
in
De Lathauwer et al. 2000
, which states
\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{m}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{M}}_{{\bm{r}}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})\|_{\rm{F}}
, where
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{M}}_{{\bm{r}}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})
is the best rank
{\bm{r}}
approximation (under Frobenius norm) of
{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}
.
Using this inequality, we get,

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|\textsf{HOSVD}_{{\bm{r}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+})-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{m}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{M}}_{{\bm{r}}}}({\bm{\mathcal{T}}})\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq(\sqrt{m}+1)\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}^{+}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\big(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\eta\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}\big).

Using
(a+b)^{4}\leq 8(a^{4}+b^{4})
, we obtain from Lemma
3
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+\eta^{4}{\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{t}}{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}).

Using
C_{1}\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\leq 1
and (
G.9
), we have

\displaystyle({\mathbb{E}}_{t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t+1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4})^{1/2}\leq C_{m}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}).

(87)

So we conclude

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t,2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}({\textsf{A}}_{t-1}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2})

(88)

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t,3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}({\textsf{B}}_{t-1}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}).

(89)

From (
86
), (
88
), and (
89
), as long as
T^{4}\geq C_{m}
, we obtain

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t}\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{4}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}){\textsf{B}}_{t-1}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}{\textsf{A}}_{t-1}+C\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

Notice
{\textsf{B}}_{0}={\mathbb{E}}_{0}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}(\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{0}^{c})
, and we have from (
87
),

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{4}}({\mathbb{E}}_{0}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4})^{1/2}\leq\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}).

Telescoping the inequality about
{\textsf{B}}_{t},{\textsf{B}}_{t-1}
and from (
84
), we get,

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{4}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t}{\textsf{B}}_{0}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1-\frac{\eta}{4}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t-1-\tau}{\textsf{A}}_{\tau}+C\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1-\frac{\eta}{4}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t-1-\tau}\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

On the one hand, if
\frac{\eta}{8}\geq\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}
,

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{8})^{t}{\textsf{B}}_{0}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1-\frac{\eta}{8})^{t-1-\tau}{\textsf{A}}_{\tau}+C\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1-\frac{\eta}{8})^{t-1-\tau}\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta}{8})^{t}\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2})+C\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1-\frac{\eta}{8})^{t-1-\tau}\big((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{\tau+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta}{8})^{t+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2},

where the last inequality uses
\frac{\eta}{8}\geq\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}
.
And therefore

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\textsf{B}}_{t}\leq C\eta^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C\eta T\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

On the other hand, if
\frac{\eta}{8}\leq\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}
,

\displaystyle{\textsf{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t}{\textsf{B}}_{0}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t-1-\tau}{\textsf{A}}_{\tau}+C\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t-1-\tau}\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t}\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2})+C\frac{T^{4}}{C_{m}}\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{t-1}(1+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t-1-\tau}\big((1-\frac{\eta}{4})^{\tau+1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+12\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq(1+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t}\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}(\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2})+C\eta\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}(1+\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}})^{t}\eta^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where in the last line we use
\frac{\eta}{8}\leq\frac{C_{m}}{T^{4}}
.
And thus

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\textsf{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\exp(\frac{C_{m}}{T^{3}})((1+\eta^{-1})\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2})+C\eta T\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\eta^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta T\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}),

(90)

where in the last line we use
C_{m}\leq T^{3}
. Therefore for either choice of
\eta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\textsf{B}}_{t}\leq C(\eta^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta T\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}).

(91)

From (
85
) and (
91
), we conclude

\displaystyle R_{T}\leq C(\eta^{-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta T\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}).

∎

G.10
Proof of Theorem
11

We shall consider online tensor linear regression. The proof for online tensor completion is similar and is thus omitted.
For simplicity, suppose the true but unknown horizon is
T=(2^{K}-1)t_{0}
for some positive integers
t_{0}
.
Denote square of the error after phase
k
by
E_{k}
, namely
E_{k}:=\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{(2^{k}-1)t_{0}}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. Then from Theorem
2
, we have

\displaystyle E_{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\frac{\eta_{k+1}}{8})^{2^{k}t_{0}}E_{k}+C_{0}\eta_{k+1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp(-\frac{\eta_{k+1}}{8}2^{k}t_{0})E_{k}+C_{0}\eta_{k+1}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

(92)

\displaystyle=2\exp(-2^{-4}t_{0}\eta_{0})E_{k}+C_{0}2^{-k-1}\eta_{0}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

Suppose
2\exp(-2^{-4}t_{0}\eta_{0})\leq\frac{1}{2}
.
Telescoping this inequality and we get

\displaystyle E_{K}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{K}\exp(-2^{-4}\eta_{0}t_{0}K)\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C_{1}\eta_{0}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

Now we consider the regret under the adaptive setting. We denote the regret occurred at phase
k
by
R_{(k)}
, and the start time of each phase by
s_{k}
, namely
s_{k}=(2^{k-1}-1)t_{0}
.
Using the notation as in the proof of Theorem
10
(see (
81
)), we have,

R_{(k)}=\sum_{t=s_{k}}^{s_{k+1}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\sum_{t=s_{k}}^{s_{k+1}-1}(\textsf{A}_{t}+\textsf{B}_{t}).

To this end, we define
\textsf{E}_{k}:={\mathbb{E}}_{\leq s_{k}-1}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{s_{k}}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
, which is the expected error at the beginning of phase
k
.
In fact, from the proof of Theorem
10
, we have

\displaystyle\textsf{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp(-\frac{\eta_{k}}{8}2^{k-1}t_{0})\textsf{E}_{k-1}+C\eta_{k}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

We set
\eta_{k}=O(2^{-k}(t_{0}\overline{\textsf{dof}})^{-1/2}\frac{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}}{\sigma})
. This gives us

\displaystyle\textsf{E}_{k}

\displaystyle\leq 4\exp(-c\frac{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\sqrt{t_{0}}}{\sqrt{\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\sigma})\textsf{E}_{k-1}+C2^{-k}(t_{0})^{-1/2}\overline{\textsf{dof}}^{1/2}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\sigma.

Since
\frac{\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\sqrt{t_{0}}}{\sqrt{\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\sigma}\geq c_{1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\textsf{E}_{k}\leq 2\cdot 4^{-k}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}.

Within each phase, similar as the proof in Theorem
10
, we can show

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\leq t}\|{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{t}-{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\eta_{k}}{8})^{t-s_{k}}\textsf{E}_{k-1}+C\eta_{k}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}.

And thus

\displaystyle R_{(k)}\leq C\frac{1}{\eta_{k}}\textsf{E}_{k-1}+2^{k}t_{0}\cdot C\eta_{k}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}\leq C\frac{1}{\eta_{k}}4^{-k}\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}^{2}+C\eta_{k}2^{k}t_{0}\overline{\textsf{dof}}\sigma^{2}

With the previously chosen
\eta_{k}
, we obtain

\displaystyle R_{(k)}\leq C\sqrt{t_{0}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\sigma\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}.

(93)

Together with (
93
), we obtain

\displaystyle R_{T}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}R_{(k)}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}O(\sqrt{t_{0}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\sigma\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}})\leq O\big(\sqrt{t_{0}\overline{\textsf{dof}}}\sigma\lambda_{{\textsf{\tiny min}}}\log(\frac{T}{t_{0}})\big).
